Use the HUD's existing small-text drawing. Cheap: one `TActorIterator` pass per frame only while the variable is 1.
- Console command `gb.BuildUp <Fire|Lightning|Ice> <Amount>` in `Core/GBDevCommands.cpp`: adds that raw build-up (through `UGBCombatLibrary::ApplyBuildUp`, player as instigator, a new event id) to every living enemy within 1500 cm of the player, and logs per enemy: id, value / threshold, phase.

## 9. Smoke script (`Core/GBDevCommands.cpp`, near the end, where it does not disturb later steps)
On the training dummy (spawned by the arena; find it by enemy id `TrainingDummy`):
- First, dedupe: on the empty fire bar, two adds of 20 with the SAME event id must leave it at 20 (times the dummy's BuildUpTaken, 1 by default): log `SMOKE elemental dedupe ok=%d`.
- Then for each element: send build-up of 30 with new event ids (through `UGBCombatLibrary::ApplyBuildUp`, player as instigator) until it starts the status (at most 10 adds). Log `SMOKE elemental <E> triggered=%d adds=%d`. For Fire also log `ignite=%d` (`UGBStatusEffectComponent::HasEffect("Ignite")`). Right after, one more add must be refused: log `blockedWhileActive=%d`. Any failure -> `UE_LOG(LogGB, Error, ...)`.
- Leave a second of game time after these steps before the next screenshot.

## 10. Tests (pure logic, new file `Tests/GBElementalTests.cpp`, plus one in `Tests/GBStatsTests.cpp`)
Each test must be able to fail. Check that no test name is a prefix of another.
- `Glyphbound.Elemental.MeterThreshold`: below the threshold no trigger; reaching it exactly triggers, the value is 0, the phase Active; adds during Active and during Immune are ignored; after StatusDuration the phase is Immune, after ImmunityDuration Building again and adds count; ImmunityDuration 0 goes straight back to Building; two configs (threshold 100 vs 160 with BuildUpTaken 0.75) need different numbers of the same add to trigger; BuildUpTaken 0 never fills.
- `Glyphbound.Elemental.MeterDecay`: nothing drains during the delay; after it the bar drains at DecayRate; one Advance of 1.0 s equals ten of 0.1 s (within 1e-3) both for a run that crosses the delay boundary and one fully after it; never below 0; an add resets the delay.
- `Glyphbound.Elemental.EventDedupe`: the same id twice adds once; the same id with a bigger amount adds only the difference; id 0 is never deduped; after 8 other ids the first one is forgotten (adds again).
- `Glyphbound.Stats.StrongestSlow`: with the existing stats API: a `MultiplyMin` 0.5 scaled by strength 1.1 gives 0.45 (Deep Freeze rank 1); add a `MultiplyMin` 0.4 -> the result is 0.4, not 0.18; add a plain `Multiply` 0.8 -> 0.32; remove the 0.4 source -> 0.36.

### Elemental build-up (owner's spec, 2026-11-12)
Every enemy has three independent bars: **fire, lightning, ice**. A valid hit of an element fills its bar; a full bar starts that element's status effect.
- **Rules for each bar:** its own threshold, duration, immunity time and decay. After a while without new build-up the bar drains little by little. When the status starts the bar goes back to zero; while the status runs that element does not build up again; after it ends there can be an immunity time before it builds up again. The three bars never affect each other. No reactions between elements yet.
- **Ignite (fire, sustained damage):** fire damage at fixed time steps for the status duration. Sources: fireball, fire wall, flamethrower (on its 0.3 s damage ticks), the ember staff, the elemental sword and a fire-enchanted gun.
- **Shock (lightning, interruption):** an electric discharge (a small hit of lightning damage) and the enemy's reaction: **stun** (stops moving and attacking for the duration, its attack is cancelled), **interrupt** (only cancels the attack it was winding up) or **none**. Sources: lightning bolt (every enemy it passes through, once each), lightning rune (enemies in its blast radius, so Expanded Sigil applies), static orb, the elemental sword and a lightning-enchanted gun.
- **Frost (ice, slow and control):** the enemy's reaction: **freeze** (cannot move or attack for the duration), **slow** (a strong slow) or **none**. Sources: the **ice floor only** among spells (low build-up on each of its ticks, so standing on it fills the bar slowly and an ice-enchanted gun finishes it faster), the elemental sword and an ice-enchanted gun. The ice wall and the ice shield build up nothing. The ice floor's own slow (50%, Deep Freeze 55/60/65%, cap 80%, QTE) is unchanged. **Slows never stack:** when the ice floor and Frost both slow an enemy, only the strongest counts.
- **Per enemy (no global multipliers by enemy type):** each enemy can set, per element, its damage taken, its build-up taken (separately, so they balance apart), its threshold, the status duration, the immunity time, the decay, and its reaction (or immunity to the status: an enemy can be immune to Shock and still take lightning damage). Values not set by an enemy fall back to defaults in `Game.json`.
- **Per source:** each spell has its own build-up per hit or per tick (`BuildUp` in `Spells.json`); each weapon has its own build-up per element (`FireBuildUp`, `LightningBuildUp`, `IceBuildUp` in `Weapons.json`). An unenchanted gun builds up nothing; the apprentice staff (arcane) builds up nothing. One shot counts once per enemy: the eight pellets of a shotgun shot give one build-up to each enemy they hit.
- **Weak point bonus:** a weapon hit on the head or on a weak point multiplies its build-up (`WeakPointBuildUpMult`), only once even if the head is also a weak point. It does not change the head damage bonus.
- **Visuals:** not decided yet (Niagara, shaders or something per enemy). The code only sends events (build-up changed, status started, status ended) that each enemy's look can use later. A debug view (`gb.ShowElemental 1` in the console) prints the bars over enemies for testing; it is not the game's indicator.
- **Removed by the owner (2026-11-12):** the small effects every elemental hit used to apply (burn on fire hits, the 40% slow on lightning hits, the enchant's chill). The bar's status effect is now the only elemental effect. The electric grenade builds up lightning instead of applying the old shock (architect).

### General
- Spells cost **mana**.
- **Health and mana regenerate on their own** (potions and the injection speed it up). **No stamina.**
- Numbers for each spell (damage, cost, cooldowns, upgrade tiers): **TBD**, given later.
- The ice shield's exact limits (duration, damage absorbed, break behavior): **TBD**.

## 7. Spell-casting quick-time event
**Status:** wanted for every spell, the enchant included; still a trial for the 4-day schedule, so it must be possible to switch it off (spells then cast at a fixed default strength).

Purpose: make magic feel more elaborate than pulling a trigger.

### Flow
1. Player casts. **Time slows** by about **90%** (world at ~10% speed). Inspired by the slowed time some games use while the player picks combat hacks.
2. **Part 1 â€” button sequence â†’ POWER.** Random sequence.
   - Keyboard & mouse: movement keys (W, A, S, D).
   - Gamepad: the four face buttons (A/B/X/Y or Cross/Circle/Square/Triangle).
3. **Part 2 â€” gesture â†’ REACH.**
   - Mouse: left-to-right, top-to-bottom, a circle, etc.
- **Head / weak point bonus per weapon** (owner, 2026-11-12): pistol x2; shotgun, rifle, staffs and sword x1.5. **Perks can raise it**: the weapon tree node "Marksman" adds +0.25 per rank (3 ranks) to every weapon, staff and sword included (provisional numbers). Spell projectiles get no head bonus unless a spell defines one.
- **Fireball shockwave:** pushes enemies away from the blast (strength scaled by QTE power).
- **Player:** 100 health (regenerates 2/s after 5 s without damage), 100 mana (6/s after 1.5 s), speed 750, dash 7 m in 0.18 s every 1.2 s.
- **QTE:** 4 keys, 1 gesture, 4 s in real time (raised from 3 s by the owner), world at 10% speed.
- **Staff:** Apprentice staff 12 damage every 0.35 s, no mana cost. Ember staff 22 fire damage every 0.6 s and builds up fire.
- **Guns:** pistol 18 damage, 12 rounds, 4 spare magazines; shotgun 8 pellets of 10, 6 rounds, strong up close; rifle automatic, 14 damage, 30 rounds, stronger far away. Headshots: pistol x2, shotgun and rifle x1.5.
- **Enemies:** past orc 60 health, melee 12; future orc 70 health, ranged 9; brute (sub-boss placeholder) 320 health, melee 28.
- **Status effects:** slow 50% for 4 s (ice floor); health potion 60 over 10 s; injection 35 at once; enchant +25% gun damage for 20 s. Burn, chill and the old shock were removed (v0.22).
- **Elemental build-up (first pass, to balance; defaults in `Game.json`, per enemy in `Enemies.json`):** threshold 100 for each bar; decay starts 2 s after the last build-up and drains 20 per second. Ignite 4 s, 6 fire damage every 0.5 s (48 in total), then 3 s immune. Shock: a 10 lightning damage discharge and a 1.2 s stun, then 4 s immune. Frost: 2.5 s freeze (or a 60% slow for enemies that resist freezing), then 5 s immune.
- **Build-up per source (first pass):** fireball 40 (25-55 with QTE power), fire wall 10 per 0.5 s tick, flamethrower 8 per 0.3 s tick, lightning bolt 35 (20-50), lightning rune 60 (35-85), static orb 8 per tick, ice floor 5 per 0.5 s tick (60 at most over its 6 s, so it never fills the bar alone), electric grenade 60. Weapons per hit: pistol 15, shotgun 25 per shot, rifle 8, elemental sword 20, ember staff 10 (fire). Weak point bonus x1.5.
- **Enemies (first pass):** the castle orc uses the defaults. The orc brute (sub-boss) takes 20% less fire damage and 25% less fire build-up, its thresholds are higher (fire 160, lightning 200, ice 180), a shock only interrupts its attack and Frost only slows it (50%). The armored orc takes 25% more lightning damage and lightning build-up and 25% less fire build-up.
- **Skill trees:** 6 nodes each, 1-2 points per rank, up to 3 ranks.
- Every spell number in `Spells.json`.

#include "Combat/GBCombatLibrary.h"

#include "AI/GBEnemyCharacter.h"
#include "Combat/GBElementalStatusComponent.h"
#include "Combat/GBStatusEffectComponent.h"
#include "Core/GBStatNames.h"
#include "Stats/GBAttributeComponent.h"
#include "Stats/GBStatsComponent.h"
#include "Engine/Engine.h"
#include "Engine/OverlapResult.h"
#include "Engine/World.h"
#include "CollisionQueryParams.h"

namespace GBCombatLocal
{
	/** The obstruction trace starts this far above the explosion origin, so the floor under it does not block it. */
	constexpr float RadialTraceLiftCm = 5.f;
}

FGBOnDamageDealt& UGBCombatLibrary::OnDamageDealt()
{
	static FGBOnDamageDealt Event;
	return Event;
}

float UGBCombatLibrary::DealDamage(AActor* Target, const FGBDamageInfo& Info, bool bAllowFriendlyFire)
{
	if (!IsValid(Target))
	{
		return 0.f;
	}

	UGBAttributeComponent* Attributes = UGBAttributeComponent::Find(Target);
	if (!Attributes)
	{
		return 0.f;
	}

	AActor* DamageInstigator = Info.Instigator.Get();
	if (!IsValid(DamageInstigator))
	{
		DamageInstigator = nullptr;
	}

	if (!CanDamage(DamageInstigator, Target))
	{
		// Friendly fire explicitly allows same-team hits; anything without attributes or dead stays immune.
		if (!bAllowFriendlyFire || !UGBAttributeComponent::Find(Target) || !IsAlive(Target))
		{
			return 0.f;
		}
	}

	FGBDamageInfo FinalInfo = Info;
	FinalInfo.Instigator = DamageInstigator;
	if (DamageInstigator)
	{
		if (const UGBStatsComponent* InstigatorStats = UGBStatsComponent::Find(DamageInstigator))
		{
			FinalInfo.Amount *= InstigatorStats->GetStat(GBStat::DamageDealt, 1.f);
		}
	}

	if (FinalInfo.Amount <= 0.f)
	{
		return 0.f;
	}

	const float Dealt = Attributes->ApplyDamage(FinalInfo);
	if (Dealt > 0.f)
	{
		OnDamageDealt().Broadcast(Target, FinalInfo, Dealt);
		ApplyBuildUp(Target, FinalInfo);
	}

	if (Dealt > 0.f && !FinalInfo.OnHitEffect.IsNone())
	{
		if (UGBStatusEffectComponent* Effects = UGBStatusEffectComponent::Find(Target))
		{
			Effects->ApplyEffect(FinalInfo.OnHitEffect, DamageInstigator, FinalInfo.EffectDurationScale, FinalInfo.EffectStrengthScale);
		}
	}

	return Dealt;
}

int32 UGBCombatLibrary::DealRadialDamage(const UObject* WorldContextObject, const FVector& Origin, float Radius, const FGBDamageInfo& Info,
	TConstArrayView<AActor*> IgnoreActors, bool bLinearFalloff)
{
	if (Radius <= 0.f || !GEngine)
	{
		return 0;
	}

	UWorld* World = GEngine->GetWorldFromContextObject(WorldContextObject, EGetWorldErrorMode::LogAndReturnNull);
	if (!World)
	{
		return 0;
	}

	FCollisionObjectQueryParams ObjectParams;
	ObjectParams.AddObjectTypesToQuery(ECC_Pawn);
	ObjectParams.AddObjectTypesToQuery(ECC_WorldDynamic);

	FCollisionQueryParams QueryParams(SCENE_QUERY_STAT(GBRadialDamage), false);
	for (AActor* Ignored : IgnoreActors)
	{
		if (Ignored)
		{
			QueryParams.AddIgnoredActor(Ignored);
		}
	}

	TArray<FOverlapResult> Overlaps;
	World->OverlapMultiByObjectType(Overlaps, Origin, FQuat::Identity, ObjectParams, FCollisionShape::MakeSphere(Radius), QueryParams);

	AActor* DamageInstigator = Info.Instigator.Get();
	if (!IsValid(DamageInstigator))
	{
		DamageInstigator = nullptr;
	}

	TSet<AActor*> Visited;
	int32 DamagedCount = 0;

	for (const FOverlapResult& Overlap : Overlaps)
	{
		AActor* Target = Overlap.GetActor();
		if (!IsValid(Target) || Visited.Contains(Target))
		{
			continue;
		}
		Visited.Add(Target);

		if (IgnoreActors.Contains(Target) || !UGBAttributeComponent::Find(Target))
		{
			continue;
		}

		if (!CanDamage(DamageInstigator, Target))
		{
			continue;
		}

		const FVector TargetLocation = Target->GetActorLocation();
		const float Distance = FVector::Dist(TargetLocation, Origin);
		if (Distance > Radius)
		{
			continue;
		}

		// Solid geometry between the blast and the target blocks the damage.
		FCollisionQueryParams TraceParams(SCENE_QUERY_STAT(GBRadialObstruction), true);
		TraceParams.AddIgnoredActor(Target);
		for (AActor* Ignored : IgnoreActors)
		{
			if (Ignored)
			{
				TraceParams.AddIgnoredActor(Ignored);
			}
		}
		FHitResult Obstruction;
		const FVector TraceStart = Origin + FVector(0.f, 0.f, GBCombatLocal::RadialTraceLiftCm);
		if (World->LineTraceSingleByChannel(Obstruction, TraceStart, TargetLocation, ECC_Visibility, TraceParams)
			&& Obstruction.bBlockingHit && Obstruction.GetActor() && Obstruction.GetActor() != Target)
		{
			continue;
		}

		FGBDamageInfo TargetInfo = Info;
		TargetInfo.HitLocation = TargetLocation;
		TargetInfo.HitDirection = (TargetLocation - Origin).GetSafeNormal();
		if (bLinearFalloff)
		{
			TargetInfo.Amount *= FMath::Clamp(1.f - Distance / Radius, 0.f, 1.f);
		}

		if (DealDamage(Target, TargetInfo) > 0.f)
		{
			++DamagedCount;
		}
	}

	return DamagedCount;
}

EGBTeam UGBCombatLibrary::GetTeam(const AActor* Actor)
{
	const UGBAttributeComponent* Attributes = UGBAttributeComponent::Find(Actor);
	return Attributes ? Attributes->Team : EGBTeam::Neutral;
}

bool UGBCombatLibrary::AreHostile(const AActor* A, const AActor* B)
{
	if (!A || !B)
	{
		return false;
	}
	return AreTeamsHostile(GetTeam(A), GetTeam(B));
}

bool UGBCombatLibrary::AreTeamsHostile(EGBTeam A, EGBTeam B)
{
	return (A == EGBTeam::Player && B == EGBTeam::Enemy) || (A == EGBTeam::Enemy && B == EGBTeam::Player);
}

bool UGBCombatLibrary::IsAlive(const AActor* Actor)
{
	if (!IsValid(Actor))
	{
		return false;
	}
	const UGBAttributeComponent* Attributes = UGBAttributeComponent::Find(Actor);
	return !Attributes || !Attributes->IsDead();
}

bool UGBCombatLibrary::CanDamage(const AActor* Instigator, const AActor* Target)
{
	if (!IsValid(Target))
	{
		return false;
	}
	const UGBAttributeComponent* TargetAttributes = UGBAttributeComponent::Find(Target);
	if (!TargetAttributes || TargetAttributes->IsDead())
	{
		return false;
	}
	if (!IsValid(Instigator))
	{
		return true;
	}
	const EGBTeam InstigatorTeam = GetTeam(Instigator);
	const EGBTeam TargetTeam = TargetAttributes->Team;
	if (InstigatorTeam == EGBTeam::Neutral || TargetTeam == EGBTeam::Neutral)
	{
		return true;
	}
	return AreTeamsHostile(InstigatorTeam, TargetTeam);
}

float UGBCombatLibrary::DistanceFalloffMultiplier(float Distance, float FalloffStart, float FalloffEnd, float NearMult, float FarMult)
{
	if (Distance <= FalloffStart)
	{
		return NearMult;
	}
	if (FalloffEnd <= FalloffStart || Distance >= FalloffEnd)
	{
		return FarMult;
	}
	const float Alpha = (Distance - FalloffStart) / (FalloffEnd - FalloffStart);
	return FMath::Lerp(NearMult, FarMult, Alpha);
}

bool UGBCombatLibrary::IsHeadshot(float HitZ, float FeetZ, float BodyHeight, float HeadFraction)
{
	if (HeadFraction <= 0.f || BodyHeight <= 0.f)
	{
		return false;
	}
	const float HeadLineZ = FeetZ + BodyHeight * (1.f - FMath::Min(HeadFraction, 1.f));
	return HitZ >= HeadLineZ;
}

FVector UGBCombatLibrary::ApplySpread(const FVector& Direction, float SpreadHalfAngleDegrees, FRandomStream& Random)
{
	const FVector Dir = Direction.GetSafeNormal();
	if (Dir.IsNearlyZero() || SpreadHalfAngleDegrees <= 0.f)
	{
		return Dir;
	}
	return Random.VRandCone(Dir, FMath::DegreesToRadians(SpreadHalfAngleDegrees));
}

bool UGBCombatLibrary::ApplyWeakPointBonus(const AActor* Target, FGBDamageInfo& Info, float WeakPointMult)
{
	Info.bHeadshot = IsWeakPointHit(Target, Info.HitLocation);
	if (!Info.bHeadshot || WeakPointMult <= 1.f)
	{
		return Info.bHeadshot;
	}
	Info.Amount *= WeakPointMult;
	return true;
}

bool UGBCombatLibrary::IsWeakPointHit(const AActor* Target, const FVector& HitLocation)
{
	const AGBEnemyCharacter* Enemy = Cast<AGBEnemyCharacter>(Target);
	return Enemy && Enemy->IsHeadshotLocation(HitLocation);
}

int32 UGBCombatLibrary::NewBuildUpEventId()
{
	static int32 NextId = 0;
	++NextId;
	if (NextId == 0)
	{
		++NextId;
	}
	return NextId;
}

float UGBCombatLibrary::ApplyBuildUp(AActor* Target, const FGBDamageInfo& Info)
{
	if (Info.BuildUp <= 0.f)
	{
		return 0.f;
	}
	const int32 Index = GBElemental::ElementIndex(Info.Element);
	if (Index < 0)
	{
		return 0.f;
	}
	AActor* Instigator = Info.Instigator.Get();
	if (IsValid(Instigator))
	{
		// Keep the pointer; null instigators still build up (CanDamage allows them).
	}
	else
	{
		Instigator = nullptr;
	}
	if (!CanDamage(Instigator, Target))
	{
		return 0.f;
	}
	UGBElementalStatusComponent* Elemental = UGBElementalStatusComponent::Find(Target);
	if (!Elemental)
	{
		return 0.f;
	}
	const float Mult = Info.bHeadshot ? FMath::Max(1.f, Info.WeakPointBuildUpMult) : 1.f;
	const float Amount = Info.BuildUp * Mult;
	Elemental->AddBuildUp(Info.Element, Amount, Info.BuildUpEventId, Instigator);
	return Amount;
}

FVector UGBCombatLibrary::ClosestBodyPointToAim(const FVector& AimOrigin, const FVector& AimDirection, float AimLength, const FVector& BodyBottom, const FVector& BodyTop)
{
	const FVector AimEnd = AimOrigin + AimDirection * AimLength;
	FVector OutAimPoint = AimOrigin;
	FVector OutBodyPoint = BodyBottom;
	FMath::SegmentDistToSegmentSafe(AimOrigin, AimEnd, BodyBottom, BodyTop, OutAimPoint, OutBodyPoint);
	return OutBodyPoint;
}
#include "Stats/GBAttributeComponent.h"

#include "Core/GBStatNames.h"
#include "GameFramework/Actor.h"
#include "Stats/GBStatsComponent.h"

namespace GBAttributePrivate
{
	// Fallbacks only for an actor whose data has no MaxHealth / MaxMana (a warning is logged).
	constexpr float FallbackMaxHealth = 100.f;
	constexpr float FallbackMaxMana = 100.f;

	float ReadStat(const UGBStatsComponent* Stats, FName Stat, float DefaultValue)
	{
		return Stats ? Stats->GetStat(Stat, DefaultValue) : DefaultValue;
	}
}

UGBAttributeComponent::UGBAttributeComponent()
{
	PrimaryComponentTick.bCanEverTick = true;
	PrimaryComponentTick.bStartWithTickEnabled = true;
}

UGBAttributeComponent* UGBAttributeComponent::Find(const AActor* Actor)
{
	return Actor ? Actor->FindComponentByClass<UGBAttributeComponent>() : nullptr;
}

UGBStatsComponent* UGBAttributeComponent::GetStats() const
{
	return UGBStatsComponent::Find(GetOwner());
}

float UGBAttributeComponent::GetMaxHealth() const
{
	return FMath::Max(0.f, GBAttributePrivate::ReadStat(GetStats(), GBStat::MaxHealth, GBAttributePrivate::FallbackMaxHealth));
}

float UGBAttributeComponent::GetMaxMana() const
{
	return FMath::Max(0.f, GBAttributePrivate::ReadStat(GetStats(), GBStat::MaxMana, GBAttributePrivate::FallbackMaxMana));
}

void UGBAttributeComponent::BeginPlay()
{
	Super::BeginPlay();

	if (UGBStatsComponent* OwnerStats = GetStats())
	{
		OwnerStats->OnStatsChanged.AddWeakLambda(this, [this]()
		{
			// Max values may have dropped (armor removed, effect ended): clamp and let the HUD redraw.
			Health = FMath::Min(Health, GetMaxHealth());
			Mana = FMath::Min(Mana, GetMaxMana());
			OnHealthChanged.Broadcast(Health, GetMaxHealth());
			OnManaChanged.Broadcast(Mana, GetMaxMana());
		});
	}

	// Safety net for owners that set their stats before BeginPlay but never call InitializeFromStats.
	const UGBStatsComponent* Stats = GetStats();
	if (!bDead && Health <= 0.f && Stats && Stats->HasBaseStat(GBStat::MaxHealth))
	{
		InitializeFromStats();
	}
}

void UGBAttributeComponent::InitializeFromStats()
{
	const UGBStatsComponent* Stats = GetStats();
	if (!Stats || !Stats->HasBaseStat(GBStat::MaxHealth))
	{
		UE_LOG(LogGB, Warning, TEXT("%s: no MaxHealth stat in the data; using %f."), *GetNameSafe(GetOwner()), GBAttributePrivate::FallbackMaxHealth);
	}

	bDead = false;
	Health = GetMaxHealth();
	Mana = GetMaxMana();
	TimeSinceDamage = 1000.f;
	TimeSinceManaSpend = 1000.f;
	OnHealthChanged.Broadcast(Health, GetMaxHealth());
	OnManaChanged.Broadcast(Mana, GetMaxMana());
}

float UGBAttributeComponent::ApplyDamage(const FGBDamageInfo& Info)
{
	if (bDead || bInvulnerable || Info.Amount <= 0.f)
	{
		return 0.f;
	}

	FGBDamageInfo Hit = Info;
	OnModifyIncomingDamage.Broadcast(Hit);

	const float Amount = FMath::Max(0.f, Hit.Amount * GBAttributePrivate::ReadStat(GetStats(), GBStat::DamageTaken, 1.f)
		* (GBElemental::ElementIndex(Hit.Element) >= 0
			? GBAttributePrivate::ReadStat(GetStats(), GBElemental::DamageTakenKey(GBElemental::ElementIndex(Hit.Element)), 1.f)
			: 1.f));
	if (Amount <= 0.f)
	{
		// Fully blocked or cancelled: not a hit, so no damage events and the regen delay keeps running.
		return 0.f;
	}

	const float Actual = FMath::Min(Amount, Health);
	Health -= Actual;
	TimeSinceDamage = 0.f;

	OnHealthChanged.Broadcast(Health, GetMaxHealth());
	OnDamaged.Broadcast(Hit, Actual);

	if (Health <= 0.f && !bDead)
	{
		Health = 0.f;
		bDead = true;
		OnDeath.Broadcast(Hit);
	}
	return Actual;
}

void UGBAttributeComponent::Heal(float Amount)
{
	if (bDead || Amount <= 0.f)
	{
		return;
	}
	const float NewHealth = FMath::Min(Health + Amount, GetMaxHealth());
	if (NewHealth != Health)
	{
		Health = NewHealth;
		OnHealthChanged.Broadcast(Health, GetMaxHealth());
	}
}

void UGBAttributeComponent::RestoreMana(float Amount)
{
	if (bDead || Amount <= 0.f)
	{
		return;
	}
	const float NewMana = FMath::Min(Mana + Amount, GetMaxMana());
	if (NewMana != Mana)
	{
		Mana = NewMana;
		OnManaChanged.Broadcast(Mana, GetMaxMana());
	}
}

bool UGBAttributeComponent::HasMana(float Amount) const
{
	return Mana >= Amount;
}

bool UGBAttributeComponent::SpendMana(float Amount)
{
	if (Amount <= 0.f)
	{
		return true;
	}
	if (Mana < Amount)
	{
		return false;
	}
	Mana -= Amount;
	TimeSinceManaSpend = 0.f;
	OnManaChanged.Broadcast(Mana, GetMaxMana());
	return true;
}

void UGBAttributeComponent::RestoreAll()
{
	bDead = false;
	Health = GetMaxHealth();
	Mana = GetMaxMana();
	OnHealthChanged.Broadcast(Health, GetMaxHealth());
	OnManaChanged.Broadcast(Mana, GetMaxMana());
}

void UGBAttributeComponent::SetHealthAndMana(float NewHealth, float NewMana)
{
	Health = FMath::Clamp(NewHealth, 0.f, GetMaxHealth());
	Mana = FMath::Clamp(NewMana, 0.f, GetMaxMana());
	// A snapshot with health revives; one at 0 leaves the owner dead (no OnDeath: nothing hit it).
	bDead = Health <= 0.f;
	OnHealthChanged.Broadcast(Health, GetMaxHealth());
	OnManaChanged.Broadcast(Mana, GetMaxMana());
}

float UGBAttributeComponent::StepRegen(float Current, float Max, float RegenPerSecond, float RegenDelay, float TimeSinceLastLoss, float DeltaTime)
{
	if (Current >= Max || RegenPerSecond <= 0.f || DeltaTime <= 0.f)
	{
		return Current;
	}
	// Only the part of this step that lies past the delay regenerates, so the result does not depend on frame rate.
	const float RegenTime = FMath::Clamp(TimeSinceLastLoss + DeltaTime - FMath::Max(0.f, RegenDelay), 0.f, DeltaTime);
	return FMath::Min(Current + RegenPerSecond * RegenTime, Max);
}

void UGBAttributeComponent::TickComponent(float DeltaTime, ELevelTick TickType, FActorComponentTickFunction* ThisTickFunction)
{
	Super::TickComponent(DeltaTime, TickType, ThisTickFunction);

	if (!bDead)
	{
		const UGBStatsComponent* Stats = GetStats();

		const float MaxHealth = GetMaxHealth();
		const float NewHealth = StepRegen(Health, MaxHealth, GBAttributePrivate::ReadStat(Stats, GBStat::HealthRegen, 0.f),
			GBAttributePrivate::ReadStat(Stats, GBStat::HealthRegenDelay, 0.f), TimeSinceDamage, DeltaTime);
		if (NewHealth != Health)
		{
			Health = NewHealth;
			OnHealthChanged.Broadcast(Health, MaxHealth);
		}

		const float MaxMana = GetMaxMana();
		const float NewMana = StepRegen(Mana, MaxMana, GBAttributePrivate::ReadStat(Stats, GBStat::ManaRegen, 0.f),
			GBAttributePrivate::ReadStat(Stats, GBStat::ManaRegenDelay, 0.f), TimeSinceManaSpend, DeltaTime);
		if (NewMana != Mana)
		{
			Mana = NewMana;
			OnManaChanged.Broadcast(Mana, MaxMana);
		}
	}

	TimeSinceDamage += DeltaTime;
	TimeSinceManaSpend += DeltaTime;
}

 succeeded in 827ms:
#include "Combat/GBStatusEffectComponent.h"

#include "Combat/GBCombatLibrary.h"
#include "Data/GBDataSubsystem.h"
#include "Stats/GBAttributeComponent.h"
#include "Stats/GBStatsComponent.h"
#include "GameFramework/Actor.h"

namespace
{
	// Float slack for timers so a tick that lands exactly on the end of the effect still runs.
	constexpr float GBEffectTimeEpsilon = 1.e-4f;

	const FGBStatusEffectDefinition* FindDefinition(const UObject* Context, FName EffectId)
	{
		const UGBDataSubsystem* Data = UGBDataSubsystem::Get(Context);
		return Data ? Data->FindStatusEffect(EffectId) : nullptr;
	}
}

UGBStatusEffectComponent::UGBStatusEffectComponent()
{
	PrimaryComponentTick.bCanEverTick = true;
	PrimaryComponentTick.bStartWithTickEnabled = true;
}

UGBStatusEffectComponent* UGBStatusEffectComponent::Find(const AActor* Actor)
{
	return Actor ? Actor->FindComponentByClass<UGBStatusEffectComponent>() : nullptr;
}

void UGBStatusEffectComponent::BeginPlay()
{
	Super::BeginPlay();

	if (UGBAttributeComponent* Attributes = UGBAttributeComponent::Find(GetOwner()))
	{
		Attributes->OnDeath.AddUObject(this, &UGBStatusEffectComponent::HandleOwnerDeath);
	}
}

void UGBStatusEffectComponent::HandleOwnerDeath(const FGBDamageInfo& KillingBlow)
{
	RemoveAllEffects();
}

bool UGBStatusEffectComponent::ApplyEffect(FName EffectId, AActor* Instigator, float DurationScale, float StrengthScale)
{
	const FGBStatusEffectDefinition* FoundDefinition = FindDefinition(this, EffectId);
	if (!FoundDefinition)
	{
		UE_LOG(LogGB, Warning, TEXT("StatusEffect: unknown effect id '%s'"), *EffectId.ToString());
		return false;
	}
	// Copy: applying damage below runs other systems' callbacks; never hold a definition pointer across them.
	const FGBStatusEffectDefinition Definition = *FoundDefinition;

	AActor* Owner = GetOwner();
	const UGBAttributeComponent* Attributes = UGBAttributeComponent::Find(Owner);
	if (!Owner || (Attributes && Attributes->IsDead()))
	{
		return false;
	}

	const float Strength = FMath::Max(0.f, StrengthScale);
	const float Duration = Definition.Duration * FMath::Max(0.f, DurationScale);

	ApplyHealthAndMana(EffectId, Definition.InstantHealth * Strength, Definition.InstantMana * Strength, Definition.Element, Instigator);

	// The instant part may have killed the owner (death clears every effect).
	if (Attributes && Attributes->IsDead())
	{
		return true;
	}
	if (Duration <= 0.f)
	{
		return true;
	}

	int32 ExistingCount = 0;
	int32 FirstIndex = INDEX_NONE;
	int32 ShortestIndex = INDEX_NONE;
	for (int32 Index = 0; Index < ActiveEffects.Num(); ++Index)
	{
		if (ActiveEffects[Index].EffectId != EffectId)
		{
			continue;
		}
		++ExistingCount;
		if (FirstIndex == INDEX_NONE)
		{
			FirstIndex = Index;
		}
		if (ShortestIndex == INDEX_NONE || ActiveEffects[Index].TimeRemaining < ActiveEffects[ShortestIndex].TimeRemaining)
		{
			ShortestIndex = Index;
		}
	}

	if (ExistingCount > 0 && Definition.bRefreshOnReapply)
	{
		RefreshEntry(FirstIndex, Duration, Strength, Instigator);
		return true;
	}
	if (ExistingCount >= FMath::Max(1, Definition.MaxStacks))
	{
		// At max stacks: renew the stack closest to running out.
		RefreshEntry(ShortestIndex, Duration, Strength, Instigator);
		return true;
	}

	FGBActiveStatusEffect& Entry = ActiveEffects.AddDefaulted_GetRef();
	Entry.EffectId = EffectId;
	Entry.TimeRemaining = Duration;
	Entry.TotalDuration = Duration;
	Entry.TimeToNextTick = Definition.TickInterval;
	Entry.StrengthScale = Strength;
	Entry.Instigator = Instigator;
	Entry.ModifierSource = FName(*FString::Printf(TEXT("Effect.%s#%d"), *EffectId.ToString(), NextEffectSerial++));
	const FName ModifierSource = Entry.ModifierSource;

	UpdateStackCount(EffectId);

	if (Definition.Modifiers.Num() > 0)
	{
		if (UGBStatsComponent* Stats = UGBStatsComponent::Find(Owner))
		{
			Stats->AddModifiers(ModifierSource, Definition.Modifiers, Strength);
		}
	}

	OnEffectsChanged.Broadcast();
	return true;
}

void UGBStatusEffectComponent::RefreshEntry(int32 Index, float Duration, float StrengthScale, AActor* EffectInstigator)
{
	if (!ActiveEffects.IsValidIndex(Index))
	{
		return;
	}

	FGBActiveStatusEffect& Entry = ActiveEffects[Index];
	Entry.TimeRemaining = Duration;
	Entry.TotalDuration = Duration;
	if (EffectInstigator)
	{
		Entry.Instigator = EffectInstigator;
	}

	if (StrengthScale > Entry.StrengthScale)
	{
		Entry.StrengthScale = StrengthScale;
		const FName EffectId = Entry.EffectId;
		const FName ModifierSource = Entry.ModifierSource;
		const FGBStatusEffectDefinition* Definition = FindDefinition(this, EffectId);
		UGBStatsComponent* Stats = UGBStatsComponent::Find(GetOwner());
		if (Definition && Stats && Definition->Modifiers.Num() > 0)
		{
			// Same source name: replaces the old modifiers with the stronger ones.
			const TArray<FGBModifierDef> Modifiers = Definition->Modifiers;
			Stats->AddModifiers(ModifierSource, Modifiers, StrengthScale);
		}
	}

	OnEffectsChanged.Broadcast();
}

void UGBStatusEffectComponent::UpdateStackCount(FName EffectId)
{
	int32 Count = 0;
	for (const FGBActiveStatusEffect& Entry : ActiveEffects)
	{
		if (Entry.EffectId == EffectId)
		{
			++Count;
		}
	}
	for (FGBActiveStatusEffect& Entry : ActiveEffects)
	{
		if (Entry.EffectId == EffectId)
		{
			Entry.Stacks = Count;
		}
	}
}

void UGBStatusEffectComponent::ApplyHealthAndMana(FName EffectId, float HealthAmount, float ManaAmount, EGBElement Element, AActor* EffectInstigator)
{
	AActor* Owner = GetOwner();
	UGBAttributeComponent* Attributes = UGBAttributeComponent::Find(Owner);
	if (!Attributes || (FMath::IsNearlyZero(HealthAmount) && FMath::IsNearlyZero(ManaAmount)))
	{
		return;
	}

	if (HealthAmount > 0.f)
	{
		Attributes->Heal(HealthAmount);
	}
	else if (HealthAmount < 0.f)
	{
		FGBDamageInfo Info;
		Info.Amount = -HealthAmount;
		Info.Element = Element;
		Info.Instigator = IsValid(EffectInstigator) ? EffectInstigator : nullptr;
		Info.HitLocation = Owner->GetActorLocation();
		Info.SourceId = EffectId;
		// OnHitEffect stays None: periodic damage must not re-apply effects.
		UGBCombatLibrary::DealDamage(Owner, Info, /*bAllowFriendlyFire*/ true);
	}

	if (Attributes->IsDead())
	{
		return;
	}

	if (ManaAmount > 0.f)
	{
		Attributes->RestoreMana(ManaAmount);
	}
	else if (ManaAmount < 0.f)
	{
		const float Drain = FMath::Min(-ManaAmount, Attributes->GetMana());
		if (Drain > 0.f)
		{
			Attributes->SpendMana(Drain);
		}
	}
}

void UGBStatusEffectComponent::RemoveEffect(FName EffectId)
{
	UGBStatsComponent* Stats = UGBStatsComponent::Find(GetOwner());
	bool bRemoved = false;
	for (int32 Index = ActiveEffects.Num() - 1; Index >= 0; --Index)
	{
		if (ActiveEffects[Index].EffectId != EffectId)
		{
			continue;
		}
		const FName ModifierSource = ActiveEffects[Index].ModifierSource;
		ActiveEffects.RemoveAt(Index);
		if (Stats)
		{
			Stats->RemoveModifiers(ModifierSource);
		}
		bRemoved = true;
	}

	if (bRemoved)
	{
		OnEffectsChanged.Broadcast();
	}
}

void UGBStatusEffectComponent::RemoveAllEffects()
{
	if (ActiveEffects.Num() == 0)
	{
		return;
	}

	TArray<FGBActiveStatusEffect> Removed = MoveTemp(ActiveEffects);
	ActiveEffects.Reset();

	if (UGBStatsComponent* Stats = UGBStatsComponent::Find(GetOwner()))
	{
		for (const FGBActiveStatusEffect& Entry : Removed)
		{
			Stats->RemoveModifiers(Entry.ModifierSource);
		}
	}

	OnEffectsChanged.Broadcast();
}

bool UGBStatusEffectComponent::HasEffect(FName EffectId) const
{
	for (const FGBActiveStatusEffect& Entry : ActiveEffects)
	{
		if (Entry.EffectId == EffectId)
		{
			return true;
		}
	}
	return false;
}

float UGBStatusEffectComponent::GetTimeRemaining(FName EffectId) const
{
	float Remaining = 0.f;
	for (const FGBActiveStatusEffect& Entry : ActiveEffects)
	{
		if (Entry.EffectId == EffectId)
		{
			Remaining = FMath::Max(Remaining, Entry.TimeRemaining);
		}
	}
	return Remaining;
}

void UGBStatusEffectComponent::TickComponent(float DeltaTime, ELevelTick TickType, FActorComponentTickFunction* ThisTickFunction)
{
	Super::TickComponent(DeltaTime, TickType, ThisTickFunction);

	if (ActiveEffects.Num() == 0)
	{
		return;
	}

	const UGBAttributeComponent* Attributes = UGBAttributeComponent::Find(GetOwner());
	if (Attributes && Attributes->IsDead())
	{
		return;
	}

	struct FPendingTick
	{
		FName EffectId;
		float Health = 0.f;
		float Mana = 0.f;
		EGBElement Element = EGBElement::Physical;
		TWeakObjectPtr<AActor> Instigator;
	};

	// Phase 1: advance timers only (no callbacks), so the array is not changed while we walk it.
	TArray<FPendingTick> PendingTicks;
	TArray<int32> ExpiredIndices;
	for (int32 Index = 0; Index < ActiveEffects.Num(); ++Index)
	{
		FGBActiveStatusEffect& Entry = ActiveEffects[Index];
		const FGBStatusEffectDefinition* Definition = FindDefinition(this, Entry.EffectId);
		if (!Definition)
		{
			UE_LOG(LogGB, Warning, TEXT("StatusEffect: effect '%s' is no longer in the data; removing it"), *Entry.EffectId.ToString());
			ExpiredIndices.Add(Index);
			continue;
		}

		// Ticks only count time inside the effect, so none run after its end.
		const float ActiveStep = FMath::Min(DeltaTime, FMath::Max(0.f, Entry.TimeRemaining));
		if (Definition->TickInterval > 0.f)
		{
			Entry.TimeToNextTick -= ActiveStep;
			while (Entry.TimeToNextTick <= GBEffectTimeEpsilon)
			{
				Entry.TimeToNextTick += Definition->TickInterval;
				FPendingTick& Pending = PendingTicks.AddDefaulted_GetRef();
				Pending.EffectId = Entry.EffectId;
				Pending.Health = Definition->HealthPerTick * Entry.StrengthScale;
				Pending.Mana = Definition->ManaPerTick * Entry.StrengthScale;
				Pending.Element = Definition->Element;
				Pending.Instigator = Entry.Instigator;
			}
		}

		Entry.TimeRemaining -= DeltaTime;
		if (Entry.TimeRemaining <= GBEffectTimeEpsilon)
		{
			ExpiredIndices.Add(Index);
		}
	}

	// Phase 2: remove expired entries and their modifiers.
	if (ExpiredIndices.Num() > 0)
	{
		UGBStatsComponent* Stats = UGBStatsComponent::Find(GetOwner());
		TArray<FName> ChangedIds;
		for (int32 i = ExpiredIndices.Num() - 1; i >= 0; --i)
		{
			const int32 Index = ExpiredIndices[i];
			const FName ModifierSource = ActiveEffects[Index].ModifierSource;
			ChangedIds.AddUnique(ActiveEffects[Index].EffectId);
			ActiveEffects.RemoveAt(Index);
			if (Stats)
			{
				Stats->RemoveModifiers(ModifierSource);
			}
		}
		for (const FName& EffectId : ChangedIds)
		{
			UpdateStackCount(EffectId);
		}
		OnEffectsChanged.Broadcast();
	}

	// Phase 3: run the ticks (they may damage, kill and clear everything).
	for (const FPendingTick& Pending : PendingTicks)
	{
		if (Attributes && Attributes->IsDead())
		{
			break;
		}
		ApplyHealthAndMana(Pending.EffectId, Pending.Health, Pending.Mana, Pending.Element, Pending.Instigator.Get());
	}
}

void AGBEnemyCharacter::InitFromDefinition(const FGBEnemyDefinition& Definition)
{
	bInitialized = true;
	bPixelArtLook = Definition.bPixelArt;
	AttackModeName = Definition.AttackMode;

	Stats->SetBaseStats(Definition.Stats);
	Attributes->InitializeFromStats();

	UCapsuleComponent* Capsule = GetCapsuleComponent();
	const float Radius = FMath::Max(Stats->GetStat(GBStat::CapsuleRadius, Capsule->GetUnscaledCapsuleRadius()), 1.f);
	const float HalfHeight = FMath::Max(Stats->GetStat(GBStat::CapsuleHalfHeight, Capsule->GetUnscaledCapsuleHalfHeight()), Radius);
	Capsule->SetCapsuleSize(Radius, HalfHeight);

	if (Definition.bPixelArt)
	{
		const FString SpriteId = Definition.Strings.FindRef(GBString::Sprite);
		if (UGBPixelSpriteLibrary* Library = UGBPixelSpriteLibrary::Get(this))
		{
			Library->SetupBillboard(Sprite, FName(*SpriteId), 2.f * HalfHeight);
		}
		SpriteBaseScale = Sprite->GetRelativeScale3D();
		Sprite->SetVisibility(true);
		BodyMesh->SetVisibility(false);
	}
	else
	{
		BodyColor = UGBDataSubsystem::ParseColor(Definition.Strings.FindRef(GBString::Color), FLinearColor::Gray);
		GBPlaceholder::SetupShape(BodyMesh, EGBShape::Cylinder, FVector(2.f * Radius, 2.f * Radius, 2.f * HalfHeight), BodyColor);
		BodyMesh->SetVisibility(true);
		Sprite->SetVisibility(false);
	}

	Stats->OnStatsChanged.RemoveAll(this);
	Stats->OnStatsChanged.AddUObject(this, &AGBEnemyCharacter::ApplyMovementStats);
	Attributes->OnDeath.RemoveAll(this);
	Attributes->OnDeath.AddUObject(this, &AGBEnemyCharacter::HandleDeath);
	Attributes->OnDamaged.RemoveAll(this);
	Attributes->OnDamaged.AddUObject(this, &AGBEnemyCharacter::HandleDamaged);

	ApplyMovementStats();
}

void AGBEnemyCharacter::HandleDataReloaded()
{
	if (const FGBEnemyDefinition* Def = GetDefinition())
	{
		AttackModeName = Def->AttackMode;
		Stats->SetBaseStats(Def->Stats);
	}
}

void AGBEnemyCharacter::ApplyMovementStats()
{
	if (UCharacterMovementComponent* Move = GetCharacterMovement())
	{
		Move->MaxWalkSpeed = FMath::Max(0.f, Stats->GetStat(GBStat::MoveSpeed, Move->MaxWalkSpeed));
	}
}

AActor* AGBEnemyCharacter::FindTarget() const
{
	AGBPlayerCharacter* Player = AGBPlayerCharacter::Get(this);
	if (Player && UGBCombatLibrary::IsAlive(Player) && UGBCombatLibrary::AreHostile(this, Player))
	{
		return Player;
	}
	return nullptr;
		GBPlaceholder::SetShapeColor(BodyMesh, Color);
	}
}

void AGBEnemyCharacter::Tick(float DeltaSeconds)
{
	Super::Tick(DeltaSeconds);

	if (State == EGBEnemyState::Dead || !EraComponent->IsPresent())
	{
		return;
	}

	if (ControlLockTime > 0.f)
	{
		ControlLockTime -= DeltaSeconds;
		ResetStuck();
		return;
	}

	StateTime += DeltaSeconds;
	NavRetryTimer = FMath::Max(0.f, NavRetryTimer - DeltaSeconds);
	SteerSideHoldTimer = FMath::Max(0.f, SteerSideHoldTimer - DeltaSeconds);
	GoAroundTimer = FMath::Max(0.f, GoAroundTimer - DeltaSeconds);
	bWantsToMove = false;

	AActor* Goal = FindTarget();
	Target = Goal;
	if (!Goal)
	{
		if (State != EGBEnemyState::Idle)
		{
			StopMoving();
			SetState(EGBEnemyState::Idle);
		}
		bProvoked = false;
		bRepositioning = false;
		ResetStuck();
		return;
	}

	const float AggroRange = Stats->GetStat(GBStat::AggroRange, 0.f);
	const float AttackRange = Stats->GetStat(GBStat::AttackRange, 0.f);
	const bool bCanAttack = AttackRange > 0.f && Stats->GetStat(GBStat::AttackDamage, 0.f) > 0.f;
	const float Distance = FVector::Dist(GetActorLocation(), Goal->GetActorLocation());

	switch (State)
	{
	case EGBEnemyState::Idle:
		// AggroRange <= 0: never acts (training dummy), even when hit.
		if (AggroRange > 0.f && (bProvoked || Distance <= AggroRange))
		{
			SetState(EGBEnemyState::Chase);
		}
		break;

	case EGBEnemyState::Chase:
		// Line of sight for melee too: an orc on the other side of a low wall keeps going around it instead of swinging at stone.
		if (bCanAttack && Distance <= AttackRange && HasLineOfSight(Goal))
		{
			StopMoving();
			FaceTowards(Goal);
			SetState(EGBEnemyState::Windup);
		}
		else
		{
			MoveTowards(Goal, DeltaSeconds);
		}
		break;

	case EGBEnemyState::Windup:
		// A shot needs a clear line: the player stepped behind cover, so reposition instead of shooting the wall.
		if (IsRanged() && !HasLineOfSight(Goal))
		{
			SetState(EGBEnemyState::Chase);
			MoveTowards(Goal, DeltaSeconds);
			break;
		}
		FaceTowards(Goal);
		if (StateTime >= Stats->GetStat(GBStat::AttackWindup, 0.f))
		{
			PerformAttack();
			if (State == EGBEnemyState::Windup)
			{
				SetState(EGBEnemyState::Recover);
			}
		}
		break;

	case EGBEnemyState::Recover:
		// Ranged enemies do not wait out the interval behind a wall: they move until they see the player again.
		if (IsRanged() && !HasLineOfSight(Goal))
		{
			MoveTowards(Goal, DeltaSeconds);
			bRepositioning = true;
		}
		else
		{
			if (bRepositioning)
			{
				StopMoving();
				bRepositioning = false;
			}
			FaceTowards(Goal);
		}
		if (StateTime >= Stats->GetStat(GBStat::AttackInterval, 0.f))
		{
			bRepositioning = false;
			SetState(EGBEnemyState::Chase);
		}
		break;

	default:
		break;
	}

	UpdateStuck(DeltaSeconds);
}
	if (State == EGBEnemyState::Dead)
	{
		Super::FellOutOfWorld(DamageType);
		return;
	}

	// Die like any other death so the encounter counts it; no drops down there.
	bFellOutOfWorld = true;
	FGBDamageInfo Info;
	Info.Amount = Attributes->GetMaxHealth() + Attributes->GetHealth() + 1.f;
	Info.SourceId = FName(TEXT("FellOutOfWorld"));
	Attributes->SetInvulnerable(false);
	Attributes->ApplyDamage(Info);
	if (State != EGBEnemyState::Dead)
	{
		// The hit was cancelled (a shield, DamageTaken 0): it still cannot come back from below the world.
		HandleDeath(Info);
	}
	SetActorHiddenInGame(true);
}

void AGBEnemyCharacter::StopMoving()
{
	if (AController* C = GetController())
	{
		C->StopMovement();
	}
	if (UCharacterMovementComponent* Move = GetCharacterMovement())
	{
		Move->StopMovementImmediately();
	}
}

void AGBEnemyCharacter::FaceTowards(const AActor* Goal)
{
	if (!Goal)
	{
		return;
	}
	const FVector Direction = (Goal->GetActorLocation() - GetActorLocation()).GetSafeNormal2D();
	if (!Direction.IsNearlyZero())
	{
		SetActorRotation(FRotator(0.f, Direction.Rotation().Yaw, 0.f));
	}
}

FVector AGBEnemyCharacter::GetChestLocation() const
{
	return GetActorLocation() + FVector::UpVector * (GetCapsuleComponent()->GetScaledCapsuleHalfHeight() * GBEnemyCharacterLocal::ChestHeightFraction);
}

bool AGBEnemyCharacter::HasLineOfSight(const AActor* Goal) const
{
	if (!Goal || !GetWorld())
	{
	}
	return false;
}

void AGBEnemyCharacter::PerformAttack()
{
	AActor* Goal = Target.Get();
	const FGBEnemyDefinition* Def = GetDefinition();
	if (!Goal || !Def || !GetWorld())
	{
		return;
	}

	const float Damage = Stats->GetStat(GBStat::AttackDamage, 0.f);
	const float AttackRange = Stats->GetStat(GBStat::AttackRange, 0.f);

	FGBDamageInfo Info;
	Info.Amount = Damage;
	Info.Element = Def->Element;
	Info.Instigator = this;
	Info.Causer = this;
	Info.SourceId = EnemyId;
	Info.OnHitEffect = FName(*Def->Strings.FindRef(GBString::OnHitEffect));

	const FName Mode(*Def->AttackMode);
	if (Mode == GBEnemyCharacterLocal::AttackModeMelee)
	{
		const UGBDataSubsystem* Data = UGBDataSubsystem::Get(this);
		const float Tolerance = Data ? Data->GetGameValue(GBEnemyKey::MeleeReachTolerance, 1.2f) : 1.2f;
		const FVector ToGoal = Goal->GetActorLocation() - GetActorLocation();
		if (ToGoal.Size() <= AttackRange * Tolerance && HasLineOfSight(Goal))
		{
			Info.HitLocation = Goal->GetActorLocation();
			Info.HitDirection = ToGoal.GetSafeNormal();
			UGBCombatLibrary::DealDamage(Goal, Info);
		}
	}
	else if (Mode == GBEnemyCharacterLocal::AttackModeRanged)
	{
		FGBProjectileConfig Config;
		Config.Damage = Info;
		Config.Damage.Causer = nullptr;
		Config.Speed = Stats->GetStat(GBStat::ProjectileSpeed, Config.Speed);
		Config.Radius = Stats->GetStat(GBStat::ProjectileRadius, Config.Radius);
		Config.MaxRange = Stats->GetStat(GBStat::AggroRange, 0.f) * 1.5f;
		Config.bPixelArt = Def->bPixelArt;
		if (Def->bPixelArt)
		{
			const FString SpriteId = Def->Strings.FindRef(GBString::ProjectileSprite);
			Config.SpriteId = FName(SpriteId.IsEmpty() ? GBEnemyCharacterLocal::DefaultEnemyProjectileSprite : *SpriteId);
		}
		else
		{
			Config.Color = UGBDataSubsystem::ParseColor(Def->Strings.FindRef(GBEnemyKey::ProjectileColor),
				GBPlaceholder::ElementColor(static_cast<uint8>(Def->Element)));
		}

		const FVector Forward = GetActorForwardVector();
		const FVector Origin = GetChestLocation() + Forward * (GetCapsuleComponent()->GetScaledCapsuleRadius() + Config.Radius);
		FVector Direction = Goal->GetActorLocation() - Origin;
		if (Direction.IsNearlyZero())
		{
			Direction = Forward;
		}
		AGBProjectile::SpawnProjectile(GetWorld(), Config, Origin, Direction, this);
	}
	else
	{
		UE_LOG(LogGB, Warning, TEXT("%s: unknown AttackMode '%s'"), *EnemyId.ToString(), *Def->AttackMode);
	}
}

bool AGBEnemyCharacter::IsHeadshotLocation(const FVector& HitLocation) const
{
	const float HalfHeight = GetCapsuleComponent()->GetScaledCapsuleHalfHeight();
	const float FeetZ = GetActorLocation().Z - HalfHeight;
	return UGBCombatLibrary::IsHeadshot(HitLocation.Z, FeetZ, 2.f * HalfHeight, Stats->GetStat(GBStat::HeadFraction, 0.f));
}

void AGBEnemyCharacter::HandleDamaged(const FGBDamageInfo& Info, float ActualDamage)
{
	if (State != EGBEnemyState::Dead)
	{
		bProvoked = true;
	}
}

void AGBEnemyCharacter::HandlePresenceChanged()
{
	ResetStuck();
	if (!EraComponent->IsPresent() && State != EGBEnemyState::Dead)
	{
		StopMoving();
		bRepositioning = false;
		if (State == EGBEnemyState::Windup)
		{
			SetState(EGBEnemyState::Chase);
		}
	}
}

void AGBEnemyCharacter::HandleDeath(const FGBDamageInfo& KillingBlow)
{
	if (State == EGBEnemyState::Dead)
	{
		return;
	}
	SetState(EGBEnemyState::Dead);
	ControlLockTime = 0.f;

	StopMoving();
	if (UCharacterMovementComponent* Move = GetCharacterMovement())
	{
		Move->DisableMovement();
	}
	DetachFromControllerPendingDestroy();
	SetActorEnableCollision(false);

	UWorld* World = GetWorld();
	const FGBEnemyDefinition* Def = GetDefinition();
	if (Def && !bFellOutOfWorld)
	{
		AGBPickup::SpawnDrops(World, Def->Drops, GetActorLocation());
	}

	OnDied.Broadcast(this);

	const UGBDataSubsystem* Data = UGBDataSubsystem::Get(this);
	const float CorpseTime = Data ? Data->GetGameValue(GBEnemyKey::CorpseTime, 1.5f) : 1.5f;
	// SetLifeSpan(0) would mean "forever"; keep a tiny minimum instead of destroying inside the damage call.
	SetLifeSpan(FMath::Max(CorpseTime, 0.05f));
}

float UGBAttributeComponent::ApplyDamage(const FGBDamageInfo& Info)
{
	if (bDead || bInvulnerable || Info.Amount <= 0.f)
	{
		return 0.f;
	}

	FGBDamageInfo Hit = Info;
	OnModifyIncomingDamage.Broadcast(Hit);

	const float Amount = FMath::Max(0.f, Hit.Amount * GBAttributePrivate::ReadStat(GetStats(), GBStat::DamageTaken, 1.f)
		* (GBElemental::ElementIndex(Hit.Element) >= 0
			? GBAttributePrivate::ReadStat(GetStats(), GBElemental::DamageTakenKey(GBElemental::ElementIndex(Hit.Element)), 1.f)
			: 1.f));
	if (Amount <= 0.f)
	{
		// Fully blocked or cancelled: not a hit, so no damage events and the regen delay keeps running.
		return 0.f;
	}

	const float Actual = FMath::Min(Amount, Health);
	Health -= Actual;
	TimeSinceDamage = 0.f;

	OnHealthChanged.Broadcast(Health, GetMaxHealth());
	OnDamaged.Broadcast(Hit, Actual);

	if (Health <= 0.f && !bDead)
	{
		Health = 0.f;
		bDead = true;
		OnDeath.Broadcast(Hit);
	}
	return Actual;
}

void UGBAttributeComponent::Heal(float Amount)
{
	if (bDead || Amount <= 0.f)
	{
		return;
	}
	const float NewHealth = FMath::Min(Health + Amount, GetMaxHealth());
	if (NewHealth != Health)
	{
		Health = NewHealth;
		OnHealthChanged.Broadcast(Health, GetMaxHealth());
	}
}

void UGBAttributeComponent::RestoreMana(float Amount)
{
	if (bDead || Amount <= 0.f)
	{
		return;
	}
	const float NewMana = FMath::Min(Mana + Amount, GetMaxMana());
	if (NewMana != Mana)
	{
		Mana = NewMana;
		OnManaChanged.Broadcast(Mana, GetMaxMana());
	}
}

bool UGBAttributeComponent::HasMana(float Amount) const
{
	return Mana >= Amount;
}

bool UGBAttributeComponent::SpendMana(float Amount)
{
	if (Amount <= 0.f)
	{
		return true;
	}
	if (Mana < Amount)
	{
		return false;
	}
	Mana -= Amount;
	TimeSinceManaSpend = 0.f;
	OnManaChanged.Broadcast(Mana, GetMaxMana());
	return true;
}

void UGBAttributeComponent::RestoreAll()
{
	bDead = false;
	Health = GetMaxHealth();
	Mana = GetMaxMana();
	OnHealthChanged.Broadcast(Health, GetMaxHealth());
	OnManaChanged.Broadcast(Mana, GetMaxMana());
}

#include "GameFramework/Actor.h"
#include "GameFramework/Pawn.h"
#include "GameFramework/PawnMovementComponent.h"
#include "World/GBEraSubsystem.h"

UGBEraComponent::UGBEraComponent()
{
	PrimaryComponentTick.bCanEverTick = false;
}

void UGBEraComponent::Configure(EGBEraPresence InPresence, FName InRequiredFlag, FName InRemovedByFlag)
{
	Presence = InPresence;
	RequiredFlag = InRequiredFlag;
	RemovedByFlag = InRemovedByFlag;
	if (HasBegunPlay())
	{
		Refresh();
	}
}

bool UGBEraComponent::ComputePresent(EGBEraPresence InPresence, EGBEra CurrentEra, bool bRequiredFlagOk, bool bRemovedFlagSet)
{
	return UGBEraSubsystem::IsPresentIn(InPresence, CurrentEra) && bRequiredFlagOk && !bRemovedFlagSet;
}

void UGBEraComponent::BeginPlay()
{
	Super::BeginPlay();

	if (UGBEraSubsystem* Era = UGBEraSubsystem::Get(this))
	{
		EraChangedHandle = Era->OnEraChanged.AddUObject(this, &UGBEraComponent::HandleEraChanged);
	}
	if (UGBProgressSubsystem* Progress = UGBProgressSubsystem::Get(this))
	{
		FlagChangedHandle = Progress->OnFlagChanged.AddUObject(this, &UGBEraComponent::HandleFlagChanged);
	}
	Refresh();
}

void UGBEraComponent::EndPlay(const EEndPlayReason::Type EndPlayReason)
{
	if (UGBEraSubsystem* Era = UGBEraSubsystem::Get(this))
	{
		Era->OnEraChanged.Remove(EraChangedHandle);
	}
	if (UGBProgressSubsystem* Progress = UGBProgressSubsystem::Get(this))
	{
		Progress->OnFlagChanged.Remove(FlagChangedHandle);
	}
	EraChangedHandle.Reset();
	FlagChangedHandle.Reset();

	Super::EndPlay(EndPlayReason);
}

void UGBEraComponent::HandleEraChanged(EGBEra NewEra)
{
	Refresh();
}

void UGBEraComponent::HandleFlagChanged(FName Flag)
{
	if (Flag == RequiredFlag || Flag == RemovedByFlag)
	{
		Refresh();
	}
}

void UGBEraComponent::Refresh()
{
	const UGBEraSubsystem* Era = UGBEraSubsystem::Get(this);
	const EGBEra CurrentEra = Era ? Era->GetCurrentEra() : EGBEra::Past;

	const UGBProgressSubsystem* Progress = UGBProgressSubsystem::Get(this);
	const bool bRequiredOk = RequiredFlag.IsNone() || (Progress && Progress->HasFlag(RequiredFlag));
	const bool bRemoved = !RemovedByFlag.IsNone() && Progress && Progress->HasFlag(RemovedByFlag);

	ApplyPresence(ComputePresent(Presence, CurrentEra, bRequiredOk, bRemoved));
}

void UGBEraComponent::ApplyPresence(bool bNewPresent)
{
	AActor* Owner = GetOwner();
	if (!Owner || bNewPresent == bPresent)
	{
		return;
	}
	bPresent = bNewPresent;

	APawn* Pawn = Cast<APawn>(Owner);
	UMovementComponent* Movement = Pawn ? Pawn->GetMovementComponent() : nullptr;

	if (!bNewPresent)
	{
		// Remember the owner's own settings (it may have turned collision or tick off itself).
		bSavedHidden = Owner->IsHidden();
		bSavedCollision = Owner->GetActorEnableCollision();
		bSavedTick = Owner->IsActorTickEnabled();
		bHasSaved = true;

		// Remember every ticking component first (movement included), so the other era simulates nothing.
		SavedTickComponents.Reset();
		TArray<UActorComponent*> Components;
		Owner->GetComponents<UActorComponent>(Components);
		for (UActorComponent* Comp : Components)
		{
			if (Comp && Comp != this && Comp->IsComponentTickEnabled())
			{
				SavedTickComponents.Add(Comp);
				Comp->SetComponentTickEnabled(false);
			}
		}

		Owner->SetActorHiddenInGame(true);
		Owner->SetActorEnableCollision(false);
		Owner->SetActorTickEnabled(false);
		// An enemy of the other era must neither fall (its collision is off) nor walk.
		if (Movement)
		{
			Movement->StopMovementImmediately();
			Movement->SetComponentTickEnabled(false);
		}
	}
	else
	{
		Owner->SetActorHiddenInGame(bHasSaved ? bSavedHidden : false);
		Owner->SetActorEnableCollision(bHasSaved ? bSavedCollision : true);
		Owner->SetActorTickEnabled(bHasSaved ? bSavedTick : true);
		// Re-enable exactly the components that were ticking (a burning enemy keeps burning on return).
		for (TWeakObjectPtr<UActorComponent>& Saved : SavedTickComponents)
		{
			if (UActorComponent* Comp = Saved.Get())
			{
				Comp->SetComponentTickEnabled(true);
			}
		}
		SavedTickComponents.Reset();
		if (Movement && !bHasSaved)
		{
			Movement->SetComponentTickEnabled(true);
		}
	}

	OnPresenceChanged.Broadcast();
}

namespace
{
	// Visual only: the fallback disc thickness when the spell has no sprite. The lift above
	// the ground and the per-layer step come from Game.json (GroundFigureLift/LayerStep).
	constexpr float ZoneDiscThicknessCm = 4.f;
	const FName FigureGlowParam(TEXT("FigureGlow"));
}

AGBSpell_Zone::AGBSpell_Zone()
{
	Figure = CreateDefaultSubobject<UStaticMeshComponent>(TEXT("Figure"));
	Figure->SetupAttachment(Root);
	Figure->SetCollisionEnabled(ECollisionEnabled::NoCollision);
	Figure->SetGenerateOverlapEvents(false);
	Figure->SetCastShadow(false);
}

void AGBSpell_Zone::StartSpell()
{
	ZoneRadius = FMath::Max(1.f, ReadParam(GBStat::Radius, 300.f));
	ZoneHeight = FMath::Max(1.f, SpellCast.Get(GBStat::Height, 200.f));
	const float Duration = ReadParam(GBStat::Duration, 5.f);
	const float Interval = SafeInterval(ReadParam(GBStat::TickInterval, 0.5f));
	DamagePerTick = FMath::Max(0.f, SpellCast.Get(GBStat::DamagePerSecond, 0.f)) * Interval;

	SetActorLocationAndRotation(FindAimedGroundPoint(SpellCast.Get(GBStat::Range, 0.f)), FRotator(0.f, SpellCast.Direction.Rotation().Yaw, 0.f));

	const FString ColorStr = SpellCast.GetString(GBString::Color);
	const FLinearColor Tint = ColorStr.IsEmpty() ? FLinearColor::White : UGBDataSubsystem::ParseColor(ColorStr, FLinearColor::White);
	const float Glow = SpellCast.Get(FigureGlowParam, 1.f);

	const FName SpriteId = GetSpriteId();
	if (SpriteId.IsNone())
	{
		GBPlaceholder::SetupShape(Figure, EGBShape::Cylinder, FVector(ZoneRadius * 2.f, ZoneRadius * 2.f, ZoneDiscThicknessCm), GetSpellColor(), true);
		PlaceGroundFigure(Figure, ZoneRadius);
		Figure->AddWorldOffset(FVector(0.f, 0.f, ZoneDiscThicknessCm * 0.5f));
		GroundFigureZ = Figure->GetComponentLocation().Z;
	}
	else
	{
		GBPlaceholder::SetupFlatSprite(Figure, SpriteId, ZoneRadius * 2.f, Tint, Glow);
		PlaceGroundFigure(Figure, ZoneRadius);
	}

	if (SpellCast.GetString(GBString::OnHitEffect).IsEmpty() && DamagePerTick <= 0.f)
	{
		UE_LOG(LogGB, Warning, TEXT("Spell '%s': zone with no OnHitEffect and no DamagePerSecond does nothing."), *SpellCast.SpellId.ToString());
	}

	GetWorldTimerManager().SetTimer(TickTimer, this, &AGBSpell_Zone::ZoneTick, Interval, true, 0.f);
	EndSpellAfter(Duration);
}

void AGBSpell_Zone::ZoneTick()
{
	TArray<AActor*> Targets;
	GatherHostilesOnDisc(GetActorLocation(), ZoneRadius, ZoneHeight, Targets);
	if (Targets.Num() == 0)
	{
		return;
	}
	if (DamagePerTick > 0.f)
	{
		for (AActor* Target : Targets)
		{
			// DealDamage also applies the on-hit effect and the build-up.
			DamageTarget(Target, DamagePerTick, GetActorLocation());
		}
	}
	else
	{
		// No damage, so DealDamage never runs: send the build-up directly (one event id per tick).
		FGBDamageInfo Info = SpellCast.MakeDamageInfo(NAME_None, this);
		for (AActor* Target : Targets)
		{
			ApplyOnHitEffect(Target);
			Info.HitLocation = Target->GetActorLocation();
			UGBCombatLibrary::ApplyBuildUp(Target, Info);
		}
	}
}
#include "Spells/GBSpell_Beam.h"
#include "Combat/GBCombatLibrary.h"
#include "Components/BillboardComponent.h"
#include "Core/GBStatNames.h"
#include "Data/GBDataSubsystem.h"
#include "Engine/World.h"
#include "Player/GBPlayerCharacter.h"
#include "Stats/GBAttributeComponent.h"
#include "TimerManager.h"
#include "UI/GBHUD.h"
#include "UI/GBPixelSpriteLibrary.h"

namespace
{
	// Visual only: how many sprites draw the cone.
	constexpr int32 BeamSpriteCount = 4;
}

AGBSpell_Beam::AGBSpell_Beam()
{
	PrimaryActorTick.bCanEverTick = true;
	PrimaryActorTick.bStartWithTickEnabled = false;
}

void AGBSpell_Beam::StartSpell()
{
	BeamRange = FMath::Max(1.f, ReadParam(GBStat::Range, 600.f));
	ConeHalfAngleDeg = FMath::Clamp(ReadParam(GBSpellParam::ConeAngle, 20.f), 1.f, 89.f);
	// Duration <= 0 (or missing) = no time limit: Get, not ReadParam, so no warning is logged.
	BeamMaxDuration = FMath::Max(0.f, SpellCast.Get(GBStat::Duration, 0.f));
	BeamMinDuration = FMath::Max(0.f, SpellCast.Get(GBSpellParam::MinDuration, 0.f));
	bHoldToSustain = SpellCast.Get(GBSpellParam::HoldToSustain, 0.f) > 0.5f;
	BeamStartTime = GetWorld() ? GetWorld()->GetTimeSeconds() : 0.0;
	InterruptFraction = SpellCast.Get(GBSpellParam::InterruptHitFraction, 0.f);
	const float Interval = SafeInterval(ReadParam(GBStat::TickInterval, 0.2f));
	DamagePerTick = FMath::Max(0.f, ReadParam(GBStat::DamagePerSecond, 30.f)) * Interval;
	ManaPerTick = FMath::Max(0.f, SpellCast.Get(GBSpellParam::ManaPerSecond, 0.f)) * Interval;

	// Sprites along the cone, growing with its width.
	const FName SpriteId = GetSpriteId();
	UGBPixelSpriteLibrary* Library = UGBPixelSpriteLibrary::Get(this);
	if (!SpriteId.IsNone() && Library)
	{
		const float NearHeight = FMath::Max(1.f, SpellCast.Get(GBSpellParam::SpriteSize, 40.f));
		const float TanHalfAngle = FMath::Tan(FMath::DegreesToRadians(ConeHalfAngleDeg));
		for (int32 Index = 0; Index < BeamSpriteCount; ++Index)
		{
			const float Along = BeamRange * (Index + 1) / (BeamSpriteCount + 1);
			UBillboardComponent* Sprite = NewObject<UBillboardComponent>(this);
			Sprite->SetupAttachment(Root);
			Sprite->RegisterComponent();
			AddInstanceComponent(Sprite);
			Sprite->SetRelativeLocation(FVector(Along, 0.f, 0.f));
			Library->SetupBillboard(Sprite, SpriteId, FMath::Max(NearHeight, 2.f * Along * TanHalfAngle));
			Sprites.Add(Sprite);
		}
	}

	FollowAim();
	SetActorTickEnabled(true);
	GetWorldTimerManager().SetTimer(TickTimer, this, &AGBSpell_Beam::DamageTick, Interval, true, 0.f);
	SubscribeCasterDamage();
	if (BeamMaxDuration > 0.f)
	{
		EndSpellAfter(BeamMaxDuration);
	}
}

bool AGBSpell_Beam::ShouldBeamContinue(bool bHoldToSustain, bool bKeyHeld, float Elapsed, float MinDuration, float MaxDuration)
{
	if (MaxDuration > 0.f && Elapsed >= MaxDuration)
	{
		return false;
	}
	if (Elapsed < MinDuration)
	{
		return true;
	}
	return !bHoldToSustain || bKeyHeld;
}

bool AGBSpell_Beam::IsInterruptingHit(float DamageTaken, float MaxHealth, float InterruptHitFraction)
{
	if (InterruptHitFraction <= 0.f || MaxHealth <= 0.f)
	{
		return false;
	}
	// Small tolerance so "30% or more" holds in float math (0.3f * 100 is 30.000001, which would miss a hit of 30).
	const float Threshold = InterruptHitFraction * MaxHealth;
	return DamageTaken >= Threshold - KINDA_SMALL_NUMBER * FMath::Max(1.f, Threshold);
}

void AGBSpell_Beam::SubscribeCasterDamage()
{
	if (InterruptFraction <= 0.f)
	{
		return;
	}
	UGBAttributeComponent* Attributes = UGBAttributeComponent::Find(GetCaster());
	if (!Attributes)
	{
		return;
	}
	UnsubscribeCasterDamage();
	BoundCasterAttributes = Attributes;
	CasterDamagedHandle = Attributes->OnDamaged.AddUObject(this, &AGBSpell_Beam::HandleCasterDamaged);
}

void AGBSpell_Beam::UnsubscribeCasterDamage()
{
	if (UGBAttributeComponent* Attributes = BoundCasterAttributes.Get())
	{
		Attributes->OnDamaged.Remove(CasterDamagedHandle);
	}
	CasterDamagedHandle.Reset();
	BoundCasterAttributes.Reset();
}

void AGBSpell_Beam::HandleCasterDamaged(const FGBDamageInfo& Info, float ActualDamage)
{
	if (bEnded || InterruptFraction <= 0.f)
	{
		return;
	}
	const UGBAttributeComponent* Attributes = UGBAttributeComponent::Find(GetCaster());
	const float MaxHealth = Attributes ? Attributes->GetMaxHealth() : 0.f;
	if (IsInterruptingHit(ActualDamage, MaxHealth, InterruptFraction))
	{
		HandleInterrupt();
	}
}

void AGBSpell_Beam::HandleInterrupt()
{
	if (bEnded)
	{
		return;
	}
	if (AGBHUD* Hud = AGBHUD::Get(this))
	{
		Hud->ShowMessage(TEXT("Interrupted!"), 1.5f);
	}
	if (AGBPlayerCharacter* Player = Cast<AGBPlayerCharacter>(GetCaster()))
	{
		const UGBDataSubsystem* Data = UGBDataSubsystem::Get(this);
		const float Duration = Data ? Data->GetGameValue(GBInterruptShake::Duration, 0.35f) : 0.35f;
		const float Amplitude = Data ? Data->GetGameValue(GBInterruptShake::Amplitude, 2.5f) : 2.5f;
		const float Frequency = Data ? Data->GetGameValue(GBInterruptShake::Frequency, 22.f) : 22.f;
		Player->StartViewShake(Duration, Amplitude, Frequency);
	}
	EndSpell();
}

void AGBSpell_Beam::EndSpell()
{
	UnsubscribeCasterDamage();
	Super::EndSpell();
}

void AGBSpell_Beam::EndPlay(const EEndPlayReason::Type EndPlayReason)
{
	UnsubscribeCasterDamage();
	Super::EndPlay(EndPlayReason);
}

void AGBSpell_Beam::CheckSustain()
{
	if (bEnded)
	{
		return;
	}
	const double Now = GetWorld() ? GetWorld()->GetTimeSeconds() : BeamStartTime;
	const float Elapsed = static_cast<float>(Now - BeamStartTime);
	bool bKeyHeld = true;
	if (const AGBPlayerCharacter* Player = Cast<AGBPlayerCharacter>(GetCaster()))
	{
		bKeyHeld = Player->IsSpellInputHeld(SpellCast.SpellId);
	}
	if (!ShouldBeamContinue(bHoldToSustain, bKeyHeld, Elapsed, BeamMinDuration, BeamMaxDuration))
	{
		EndSpell();
	}
}

void AGBSpell_Beam::Tick(float DeltaSeconds)
{
	Super::Tick(DeltaSeconds);
	if (!bEnded)
	{
		FollowAim();
		CheckSustain();
	}
}

void AGBSpell_Beam::FollowAim()
{
	FVector AimOrigin;
	FVector AimDir;
	GetCasterAim(AimOrigin, AimDir);
	FVector Start = AimOrigin;
	if (const AGBPlayerCharacter* Player = Cast<AGBPlayerCharacter>(GetCaster()))
	{
		Start = Player->GetMuzzleLocation();
	}
	SetActorLocationAndRotation(Start, AimDir.Rotation());
}

void AGBSpell_Beam::DamageTick()
{
	AActor* Caster = GetCaster();
	if (!Caster || !UGBCombatLibrary::IsAlive(Caster))
	{
		EndSpell();
		return;
	}
	CheckSustain();
	if (bEnded)
	{
		return;
	}
	if (ManaPerTick > 0.f)
	{
		UGBAttributeComponent* Attributes = UGBAttributeComponent::Find(Caster);
		if (!Attributes || !Attributes->SpendMana(ManaPerTick))
		{
			EndSpell();
			return;
		}
	}
	if (DamagePerTick <= 0.f)
	{
		return;
	}

	FVector AimOrigin;
	FVector AimDir;
	GetCasterAim(AimOrigin, AimDir);
	const float MinDot = FMath::Cos(FMath::DegreesToRadians(ConeHalfAngleDeg));

	FCollisionQueryParams Params(SCENE_QUERY_STAT(GBSpellBeamSight), false, this);
	Params.AddIgnoredActor(Caster);

	TArray<AActor*> Candidates;
	GatherHostilesInSphere(AimOrigin, BeamRange, Candidates);
	for (AActor* Target : Candidates)
	{
		const FVector TargetLocation = Target->GetActorLocation();
		const FVector ToTarget = TargetLocation - AimOrigin;
		const float Distance = ToTarget.Size();
		if (Distance > KINDA_SMALL_NUMBER && FVector::DotProduct(ToTarget / Distance, AimDir) < MinDot)
		{
			continue;
		}
		if (GetWorld()->LineTraceTestByObjectType(AimOrigin, TargetLocation, FCollisionObjectQueryParams(ECC_WorldStatic), Params))
		{
			continue;
		}
		DamageTarget(Target, DamagePerTick, AimOrigin);
	}
}

 succeeded in 1142ms:
#include "Combat/GBProjectile.h"

#include "AI/GBEnemyCharacter.h"
#include "Combat/GBCombatLibrary.h"
#include "Combat/GBExplosionEffect.h"
#include "Core/GBPlaceholder.h"
#include "Stats/GBAttributeComponent.h"
#include "UI/GBPixelSpriteLibrary.h"
#include "CollisionQueryParams.h"
#include "Components/BillboardComponent.h"
#include "Components/SphereComponent.h"
#include "Components/StaticMeshComponent.h"
#include "Engine/OverlapResult.h"
#include "Engine/World.h"
#include "GameFramework/Character.h"
#include "GameFramework/Pawn.h"
#include "GameFramework/ProjectileMovementComponent.h"

namespace GBProjectileLocal
{
	/** Same lift as the radial damage obstruction trace (GBCombatLibrary.cpp), so a blast on the floor is not blocked by it. */
	constexpr float KnockbackTraceLiftCm = 5.f;
	/**
	 * Smallest upward part of the push direction. A walking character pushed only sideways is stopped at once by
	 * ground friction; a little lift makes it fly, so the push is felt even for a blast at the target's height.
	 */
	constexpr float KnockbackMinLift = 0.35f;
}

AGBProjectile::AGBProjectile()
{
	PrimaryActorTick.bCanEverTick = true;

	Collision = CreateDefaultSubobject<USphereComponent>(TEXT("Collision"));
	Collision->SetCollisionEnabled(ECollisionEnabled::QueryOnly);
	Collision->SetCollisionObjectType(ECC_WorldDynamic);
	Collision->SetCollisionResponseToAllChannels(ECR_Ignore);
	Collision->SetCollisionResponseToChannel(ECC_Pawn, ECR_Overlap);
	Collision->SetCollisionResponseToChannel(ECC_WorldDynamic, ECR_Overlap);
	Collision->SetCollisionResponseToChannel(ECC_WorldStatic, ECR_Block);
	Collision->SetGenerateOverlapEvents(true);
	Collision->SetCanEverAffectNavigation(false);
	Collision->OnComponentHit.AddDynamic(this, &AGBProjectile::OnCollisionHit);
	Collision->OnComponentBeginOverlap.AddDynamic(this, &AGBProjectile::OnCollisionOverlap);
	RootComponent = Collision;

	Movement = CreateDefaultSubobject<UProjectileMovementComponent>(TEXT("Movement"));
	Movement->UpdatedComponent = Collision;
	Movement->bRotationFollowsVelocity = true;
	// The launch velocity is given in world space by InitProjectile.
	Movement->bInitialVelocityInLocalSpace = false;
	Movement->bShouldBounce = false;
	Movement->ProjectileGravityScale = 0.f;

	Sprite = CreateDefaultSubobject<UBillboardComponent>(TEXT("Sprite"));
	Sprite->SetupAttachment(Collision);

	Mesh = CreateDefaultSubobject<UStaticMeshComponent>(TEXT("Mesh"));
	Mesh->SetupAttachment(Collision);
	Mesh->SetCollisionEnabled(ECollisionEnabled::NoCollision);
	Mesh->SetGenerateOverlapEvents(false);
	Mesh->SetCastShadow(false);
	Mesh->SetCanEverAffectNavigation(false);
}

AGBProjectile* AGBProjectile::SpawnProjectile(UWorld* World, const FGBProjectileConfig& InConfig, const FVector& Origin, const FVector& Direction, AActor* InInstigator)
{
	if (!World)
	{
		return nullptr;
	}

	const FVector LaunchDirection = Direction.IsNearlyZero() ? FVector::ForwardVector : Direction.GetSafeNormal();
	const FTransform SpawnTransform(LaunchDirection.Rotation(), Origin);

	AGBProjectile* Projectile = World->SpawnActorDeferred<AGBProjectile>(AGBProjectile::StaticClass(), SpawnTransform, InInstigator,
		Cast<APawn>(InInstigator), ESpawnActorCollisionHandlingMethod::AlwaysSpawn);
	if (!Projectile)
	{
		UE_LOG(LogGB, Warning, TEXT("Projectile: spawn failed"));
		return nullptr;
	}

	Projectile->InitProjectile(InConfig, LaunchDirection, InInstigator);
	Projectile->FinishSpawning(SpawnTransform);

	if (IsValid(InInstigator) && IsValid(Projectile))
	{
		Projectile->Collision->IgnoreActorWhenMoving(InInstigator, true);
		if (UPrimitiveComponent* InstigatorRoot = Cast<UPrimitiveComponent>(InInstigator->GetRootComponent()))
		{
			InstigatorRoot->IgnoreActorWhenMoving(Projectile, true);
		}
	}

	return IsValid(Projectile) ? Projectile : nullptr;
}

void AGBProjectile::InitProjectile(const FGBProjectileConfig& InConfig, const FVector& Direction, AActor* InInstigator)
{
	Config = InConfig;
	if (InInstigator)
	{
		Config.Damage.Instigator = InInstigator;
	}
	Config.Damage.Causer = this;
	StartLocation = GetActorLocation();

	const float Radius = FMath::Max(Config.Radius, 1.f);
	Collision->SetSphereRadius(Radius);

	Movement->InitialSpeed = Config.Speed;
	Movement->MaxSpeed = Config.Speed;
	Movement->ProjectileGravityScale = Config.GravityScale;
	Movement->bShouldBounce = Config.FuseTime > 0.f;
	Movement->Velocity = Direction.GetSafeNormal() * Config.Speed;

	const float VisualSize = Radius * 2.f * FMath::Max(Config.VisualScale, 0.01f);
	if (Config.bPixelArt)
	{
		Mesh->SetVisibility(false);
		if (UGBPixelSpriteLibrary* SpriteLibrary = UGBPixelSpriteLibrary::Get(this))
		{
			SpriteLibrary->SetupBillboard(Sprite, Config.SpriteId, VisualSize);
		}
		else
		{
			UE_LOG(LogGB, Warning, TEXT("Projectile: no pixel sprite library; sprite '%s' not shown"), *Config.SpriteId.ToString());
		}
	}
	else
	{
		Sprite->SetVisibility(false);
		Sprite->SetHiddenInGame(true);
		GBPlaceholder::SetupShape(Mesh, EGBShape::Sphere, FVector(VisualSize), Config.Color, /*bGlow*/ true);
	}
}

void AGBProjectile::BeginPlay()
{
	Super::BeginPlay();
}

void AGBProjectile::EndPlay(const EEndPlayReason::Type EndPlayReason)
{
	// Undo the instigator's ignore entry so its list does not grow with every shot.
	AActor* ProjectileInstigator = Config.Damage.Instigator.Get();
	if (IsValid(ProjectileInstigator))
	{
		if (UPrimitiveComponent* InstigatorRoot = Cast<UPrimitiveComponent>(ProjectileInstigator->GetRootComponent()))
		{
			InstigatorRoot->IgnoreActorWhenMoving(this, false);
		}
	}

	Super::EndPlay(EndPlayReason);
}

void AGBProjectile::Tick(float DeltaSeconds)
{
	Super::Tick(DeltaSeconds);

	if (bFinished)
	{
		return;
	}

	const FVector Location = GetActorLocation();
	const float Age = GetGameTimeSinceCreation();
	const bool bOutOfRange = Config.MaxRange > 0.f && FVector::Dist(StartLocation, Location) >= Config.MaxRange;
	const bool bTooOld = Config.MaxLifetime > 0.f && Age > Config.MaxLifetime;
	const bool bFuseDone = Config.FuseTime > 0.f && Age >= Config.FuseTime;

	if (bOutOfRange || bTooOld || bFuseDone)
	{
		if (Config.ExplosionRadius > 0.f)
		{
			Explode(Location);
		}
		Finish(nullptr, Location);
	}
}

bool AGBProjectile::IsValidTarget(const AActor* Other) const
{
	if (!IsValid(Other) || Other == this || Other->IsA<AGBProjectile>())
	{
		return false;
	}

	const AActor* ProjectileInstigator = Config.Damage.Instigator.Get();
	if (Other == ProjectileInstigator)
	{
		return false;
	}

	if (!UGBCombatLibrary::CanDamage(ProjectileInstigator, Other))
	{
		return false;
	}

	for (const TWeakObjectPtr<AActor>& Damaged : DamagedActors)
	{
		if (Damaged.Get() == Other)
		{
			return false;
		}
	}
	return true;
}

void AGBProjectile::HitTarget(AActor* Other, const FVector& ImpactPoint)
{
	DamagedActors.Add(Other);

	if (Config.ExplosionRadius > 0.f)
	{
		Explode(ImpactPoint);
	}
	else
	{
		FGBDamageInfo Info = Config.Damage;
		Info.Causer = this;
		Info.HitLocation = ImpactPoint;
		Info.HitDirection = Movement->Velocity.GetSafeNormal();
		// Only shots with a head bonus (damage or build-up) look for the weak point: a spell bolt on the
		// head is a normal hit, so its damage number is not shown as a headshot.
		if (Config.HeadshotMult > 1.f || Info.WeakPointBuildUpMult > 1.f)
		{
			UGBCombatLibrary::ApplyWeakPointBonus(Other, Info, Config.HeadshotMult);
		}
		UGBCombatLibrary::DealDamage(Other, Info);
	}

	// Neutral things with attributes (ice walls, destructibles) stop the shot even when it pierces.
	const bool bHitNeutral = UGBCombatLibrary::GetTeam(Other) == EGBTeam::Neutral;
	if (!Config.bPierce || bHitNeutral)
	{
		Finish(Other, ImpactPoint);
	}
}

void AGBProjectile::OnCollisionOverlap(UPrimitiveComponent* OverlappedComponent, AActor* OtherActor, UPrimitiveComponent* OtherComp,
	int32 OtherBodyIndex, bool bFromSweep, const FHitResult& SweepResult)
{
	if (bFinished || !IsValidTarget(OtherActor))
	{
		return;
	}

	const FVector ImpactPoint = (bFromSweep && !SweepResult.bStartPenetrating) ? FVector(SweepResult.ImpactPoint) : GetActorLocation();
	HitTarget(OtherActor, ImpactPoint);
}

void AGBProjectile::OnCollisionHit(UPrimitiveComponent* HitComponent, AActor* OtherActor, UPrimitiveComponent* OtherComp,
	FVector NormalImpulse, const FHitResult& Hit)
{
	if (bFinished)
	{
		return;
	}

	const FVector ImpactPoint = Hit.ImpactPoint;

	if (IsValidTarget(OtherActor))
	{
		HitTarget(OtherActor, ImpactPoint);
		return;
	}

	// The world: grenades keep bouncing until the fuse ends.
	if (Config.FuseTime > 0.f)
	{
		return;
	}

	if (Config.ExplosionRadius > 0.f)
	{
		Explode(ImpactPoint);
	}
	Finish(OtherActor, ImpactPoint);
}

void AGBProjectile::Explode(const FVector& Origin)
{
	FGBDamageInfo Info = Config.Damage;
	Info.Causer = this;
	UGBCombatLibrary::DealRadialDamage(this, Origin, Config.ExplosionRadius, Info, TConstArrayView<AActor*>(), Config.bExplosionFalloff);

	// Seen and felt even when the blast hits nothing.
	AGBExplosionEffect::Spawn(GetWorld(), Origin, Config.ExplosionRadius, Config.Color, Config.ExplosionEffectTime);
	if (Config.ExplosionKnockback > 0.f)
	{
		ApplyExplosionKnockback(Origin);
	}
}

void AGBProjectile::ApplyExplosionKnockback(const FVector& Origin)
{
	UWorld* World = GetWorld();
	const float Radius = Config.ExplosionRadius;
	if (!World || Radius <= 0.f)
	{
		return;
	}

	FCollisionObjectQueryParams ObjectParams;
	ObjectParams.AddObjectTypesToQuery(ECC_Pawn);
	FCollisionQueryParams QueryParams(SCENE_QUERY_STAT(GBExplosionKnockback), false, this);
	TArray<FOverlapResult> Overlaps;
	World->OverlapMultiByObjectType(Overlaps, Origin, FQuat::Identity, ObjectParams, FCollisionShape::MakeSphere(Radius), QueryParams);

	AActor* ProjectileInstigator = Config.Damage.Instigator.Get();
	if (!IsValid(ProjectileInstigator))
	{
		ProjectileInstigator = nullptr;
	}

	TSet<ACharacter*> Visited;
	for (const FOverlapResult& Overlap : Overlaps)
	{
		ACharacter* Character = Cast<ACharacter>(Overlap.GetActor());
		if (!IsValid(Character) || Character == ProjectileInstigator || Visited.Contains(Character))
		{
			continue;
		}
		Visited.Add(Character);

		if (!UGBCombatLibrary::CanDamage(ProjectileInstigator, Character))
		{
			continue;
		}

		const FVector TargetLocation = Character->GetActorLocation();
		const float Distance = FVector::Dist(TargetLocation, Origin);
		if (Distance > Radius)
		{
			continue;
		}

		// Same rule as the blast damage: solid geometry between the blast and the target stops the push.
		FCollisionQueryParams TraceParams(SCENE_QUERY_STAT(GBKnockbackObstruction), true, this);
		TraceParams.AddIgnoredActor(Character);
		FHitResult Obstruction;
		const FVector TraceStart = Origin + FVector(0.f, 0.f, GBProjectileLocal::KnockbackTraceLiftCm);
		if (World->LineTraceSingleByChannel(Obstruction, TraceStart, TargetLocation, ECC_Visibility, TraceParams)
			&& Obstruction.bBlockingHit && Obstruction.GetActor() && Obstruction.GetActor() != Character)
		{
			continue;
		}

		const float Strength = Config.ExplosionKnockback * FMath::Clamp(1.f - Distance / Radius, 0.f, 1.f);
		if (Strength <= 0.f)
		{
			continue;
		}

		FVector Direction = (TargetLocation - Origin).GetSafeNormal();
		if (Direction.IsNearlyZero())
		{
			// Blast right on the target: push it back along the projectile's flight.
			Direction = Movement ? Movement->Velocity.GetSafeNormal() : FVector::ZeroVector;
		}
		Direction.Z = FMath::Max(Direction.Z, GBProjectileLocal::KnockbackMinLift);
		Direction = Direction.GetSafeNormal();
		Character->LaunchCharacter(Direction * Strength, /*bXYOverride*/ true, /*bZOverride*/ true);
	}
}

void AGBProjectile::Finish(AActor* HitActor, const FVector& Location)
{
	if (bFinished)
	{
		return;
	}
	bFinished = true;

	if (Movement)
	{
		Movement->StopMovementImmediately();
	}
	OnImpact.Broadcast(this, HitActor, Location);
	Destroy();
}
