#include "Spells/GBSpell_Zone.h"
#include "Combat/GBCombatLibrary.h"
#include "Components/StaticMeshComponent.h"
#include "Core/GBPlaceholder.h"
#include "Core/GBStatNames.h"
#include "Data/GBDataSubsystem.h"
#include "TimerManager.h"

namespace
{
	// Visual only: the fallback disc thickness when the spell has no sprite. The lift above
	// the ground and the per-layer step come from Game.json (GroundFigureLift/LayerStep).
	constexpr float ZoneDiscThicknessCm = 4.f;
	const FName FigureGlowParam(TEXT("FigureGlow"));
}

AGBSpell_Zone::AGBSpell_Zone()
{
	Figure = CreateDefaultSubobject<UStaticMeshComponent>(TEXT("Figure"));
	Figure->SetupAttachment(Root);
	Figure->SetCollisionEnabled(ECollisionEnabled::NoCollision);
	Figure->SetGenerateOverlapEvents(false);
	Figure->SetCastShadow(false);
}

void AGBSpell_Zone::StartSpell()
{
	ZoneRadius = FMath::Max(1.f, ReadParam(GBStat::Radius, 300.f));
	ZoneHeight = FMath::Max(1.f, SpellCast.Get(GBStat::Height, 200.f));
	const float Duration = ReadParam(GBStat::Duration, 5.f);
	const float Interval = SafeInterval(ReadParam(GBStat::TickInterval, 0.5f));
	DamagePerTick = FMath::Max(0.f, SpellCast.Get(GBStat::DamagePerSecond, 0.f)) * Interval;

	SetActorLocationAndRotation(FindAimedGroundPoint(SpellCast.Get(GBStat::Range, 0.f)), FRotator(0.f, SpellCast.Direction.Rotation().Yaw, 0.f));

	const FString ColorStr = SpellCast.GetString(GBString::Color);
	const FLinearColor Tint = ColorStr.IsEmpty() ? FLinearColor::White : UGBDataSubsystem::ParseColor(ColorStr, FLinearColor::White);
	const float Glow = SpellCast.Get(FigureGlowParam, 1.f);

	const FName SpriteId = GetSpriteId();
	if (SpriteId.IsNone())
	{
		GBPlaceholder::SetupShape(Figure, EGBShape::Cylinder, FVector(ZoneRadius * 2.f, ZoneRadius * 2.f, ZoneDiscThicknessCm), GetSpellColor(), true);
		PlaceGroundFigure(Figure, ZoneRadius);
		Figure->AddWorldOffset(FVector(0.f, 0.f, ZoneDiscThicknessCm * 0.5f));
		GroundFigureZ = Figure->GetComponentLocation().Z;
	}
	else
	{
		GBPlaceholder::SetupFlatSprite(Figure, SpriteId, ZoneRadius * 2.f, Tint, Glow);
		PlaceGroundFigure(Figure, ZoneRadius);
	}

	if (SpellCast.GetString(GBString::OnHitEffect).IsEmpty() && DamagePerTick <= 0.f)
	{
		UE_LOG(LogGB, Warning, TEXT("Spell '%s': zone with no OnHitEffect and no DamagePerSecond does nothing."), *SpellCast.SpellId.ToString());
	}

	GetWorldTimerManager().SetTimer(TickTimer, this, &AGBSpell_Zone::ZoneTick, Interval, true, 0.f);
	EndSpellAfter(Duration);
}

void AGBSpell_Zone::ZoneTick()
{
	TArray<AActor*> Targets;
	GatherHostilesOnDisc(GetActorLocation(), ZoneRadius, ZoneHeight, Targets);
	if (Targets.Num() == 0)
	{
		return;
	}
	if (DamagePerTick > 0.f)
	{
		for (AActor* Target : Targets)
		{
			// DealDamage also applies the on-hit effect and the build-up.
			DamageTarget(Target, DamagePerTick, GetActorLocation());
		}
	}
	else
	{
		// No damage, so DealDamage never runs: send the build-up directly (one event id per tick).
		FGBDamageInfo Info = SpellCast.MakeDamageInfo(NAME_None, this);
		for (AActor* Target : Targets)
		{
			ApplyOnHitEffect(Target);
			Info.HitLocation = Target->GetActorLocation();
			UGBCombatLibrary::ApplyBuildUp(Target, Info);
		}
	}
}
#include "Stats/GBStatsComponent.h"

#include "GameFramework/Actor.h"

UGBStatsComponent::UGBStatsComponent()
{
	PrimaryComponentTick.bCanEverTick = false;
}

UGBStatsComponent* UGBStatsComponent::Find(const AActor* Actor)
{
	return Actor ? Actor->FindComponentByClass<UGBStatsComponent>() : nullptr;
}

void UGBStatsComponent::SetBaseStats(const TMap<FName, float>& InBaseStats)
{
	BaseStats = InBaseStats;
	OnStatsChanged.Broadcast();
}

void UGBStatsComponent::SetBaseStat(FName Stat, float Value)
{
	BaseStats.Add(Stat, Value);
	OnStatsChanged.Broadcast();
}

bool UGBStatsComponent::HasBaseStat(FName Stat) const
{
	return BaseStats.Contains(Stat);
}

float UGBStatsComponent::GetStat(FName Stat, float DefaultValue) const
{
	const float* Base = BaseStats.Find(Stat);
	return Evaluate(Stat, Base ? *Base : DefaultValue);
}

float UGBStatsComponent::Evaluate(FName Key, float Base) const
{
	return EvaluateKeys(MakeArrayView(&Key, 1), Base);
}

float UGBStatsComponent::EvaluateKeys(TConstArrayView<FName> Keys, float Base) const
{
	TArray<FGBModifierDef, TInlineAllocator<8>> Matching;
	for (const FSourcedModifier& Entry : ActiveModifiers)
	{
		if (Keys.Contains(Entry.Modifier.Stat))
		{
			Matching.Add(Entry.Modifier);
		}
	}
	return ApplyModifiers(Base, Matching);
}

void UGBStatsComponent::AddModifiers(FName Source, TConstArrayView<FGBModifierDef> Modifiers, float ValueScale)
{
	ActiveModifiers.RemoveAll([Source](const FSourcedModifier& Entry) { return Entry.Source == Source; });

	for (const FGBModifierDef& Modifier : Modifiers)
	{
		FSourcedModifier& Entry = ActiveModifiers.AddDefaulted_GetRef();
		Entry.Source = Source;
		Entry.Modifier = Modifier;
		switch (Modifier.Op)
		{
		case EGBModOp::Add:
			Entry.Modifier.Value = Modifier.Value * ValueScale;
			break;
		case EGBModOp::Multiply:
			// Scaled around 1, so a x1.5 buff at half strength is x1.25, not x0.75.
			Entry.Modifier.Value = 1.f + (Modifier.Value - 1.f) * ValueScale;
			break;
		case EGBModOp::MultiplyMin:
			// Same scaling as Multiply: the strongest slow at partial strength stays a slow.
			Entry.Modifier.Value = 1.f + (Modifier.Value - 1.f) * ValueScale;
			break;
		case EGBModOp::Override:
			break;
		}
	}

	OnStatsChanged.Broadcast();
}

void UGBStatsComponent::RemoveModifiers(FName Source)
{
	if (ActiveModifiers.RemoveAll([Source](const FSourcedModifier& Entry) { return Entry.Source == Source; }) > 0)
	{
		OnStatsChanged.Broadcast();
	}
}

bool UGBStatsComponent::HasModifierSource(FName Source) const
{
	return ActiveModifiers.ContainsByPredicate([Source](const FSourcedModifier& Entry) { return Entry.Source == Source; });
}

float UGBStatsComponent::ApplyModifiers(float Base, TConstArrayView<FGBModifierDef> Modifiers)
{
	float Sum = Base;
	float Product = 1.f;
	float LowestMin = 1.f;
	bool bHasMin = false;
	const FGBModifierDef* LastOverride = nullptr;

	for (const FGBModifierDef& Modifier : Modifiers)
	{
		switch (Modifier.Op)
		{
		case EGBModOp::Add:
			Sum += Modifier.Value;
			break;
		case EGBModOp::Multiply:
			Product *= Modifier.Value;
			break;
		case EGBModOp::MultiplyMin:
			if (!bHasMin || Modifier.Value < LowestMin)
			{
				LowestMin = Modifier.Value;
				bHasMin = true;
			}
			break;
		case EGBModOp::Override:
			LastOverride = &Modifier;
			break;
		}
	}

	return LastOverride ? LastOverride->Value : Sum * Product * (bHasMin ? LowestMin : 1.f);
}

// Combat logic of the elemental build-up (GDD 6): three independent bars (fire, lightning, ice).
// No visuals here: looks bind to the events. Ticks only while a bar is not empty.
#pragma once

#include "CoreMinimal.h"
#include "Components/ActorComponent.h"
#include "Core/GBTypes.h"
#include "Combat/GBBuildUpMeter.h"
#include "GBElementalStatusComponent.generated.h"

DECLARE_MULTICAST_DELEGATE_ThreeParams(FGBOnBuildUpChanged, EGBElement /*Element*/, float /*Value*/, float /*Threshold*/);
DECLARE_MULTICAST_DELEGATE_ThreeParams(FGBOnElementalStatusStarted, EGBElement /*Element*/, FName /*Reaction*/, float /*Duration*/);
DECLARE_MULTICAST_DELEGATE_OneParam(FGBOnElementalStatusEnded, EGBElement /*Element*/);

UCLASS(ClassGroup = (Glyphbound), meta = (BlueprintSpawnableComponent))
class GLYPHBOUND_API UGBElementalStatusComponent : public UActorComponent
{
	GENERATED_BODY()

public:
	UGBElementalStatusComponent();

	static UGBElementalStatusComponent* Find(const AActor* Actor);

	/**
	 * Raw build-up of Element (Fire / Lightning / Ice; others ignored), before the owner's BuildUpTaken.
	 * EventId != 0 is deduped through FGBBuildUpEventLog. Returns true if it started the status.
	 */
	bool AddBuildUp(EGBElement Element, float Amount, int32 EventId, AActor* Instigator);

	float GetBuildUp(EGBElement Element) const;
	float GetThreshold(EGBElement Element) const;
	EGBBuildUpPhase GetPhase(EGBElement Element) const;
	float GetPhaseTimeRemaining(EGBElement Element) const;
	bool IsStatusActive(EGBElement Element) const;
	FName GetReaction(EGBElement Element) const;
	void ClearAll();

	FGBOnBuildUpChanged OnBuildUpChanged;
	FGBOnElementalStatusStarted OnStatusStarted;
	FGBOnElementalStatusEnded OnStatusEnded;
	FGBOnElementalStatusEnded OnImmunityEnded;

	virtual void TickComponent(float DeltaTime, ELevelTick TickType, FActorComponentTickFunction* ThisTickFunction) override;

protected:
	virtual void BeginPlay() override;
	virtual void EndPlay(const EEndPlayReason::Type EndPlayReason) override;

private:
	bool ReadConfig(int32 Index, FGBBuildUpConfig& OutConfig, FName& OutReaction) const;
	void StartStatus(int32 Index, const FGBBuildUpConfig& Config, FName Reaction, float Duration, AActor* Instigator);
	void EndStatus(int32 Index);
	void UpdateTickEnabled();
	void HandleOwnerDeath(const FGBDamageInfo& KillingBlow);

	FGBBuildUpMeter Meters[3];
	FGBBuildUpEventLog EventLog;

	/** Enemy ids (with element) already warned about an unknown reaction: "EnemyId:Element". */
	mutable TSet<FName> WarnedReactions;
	/** Game.json keys already warned about a missing default. */
	mutable TSet<FName> WarnedDefaults;

	FDelegateHandle DeathHandle;
};

#include "Spells/GBSpell_Trap.h"
#include "Combat/GBCombatLibrary.h"
#include "Components/StaticMeshComponent.h"
#include "Core/GBPlaceholder.h"
#include "Core/GBStatNames.h"
#include "Data/GBDataSubsystem.h"
#include "TimerManager.h"

namespace
{
	// Visual only: the fallback disc thickness when the spell has no sprite. The lift above
	// the ground and the per-layer step come from Game.json (GroundFigureLift/LayerStep).
	constexpr float TrapDiscThicknessCm = 3.f;
	/** Glow brightness of the trigger area while the rune is not armed yet. */
	constexpr float TrapUnarmedBrightness = 0.3f;
}

AGBSpell_Trap::AGBSpell_Trap()
{
	PrimaryActorTick.bCanEverTick = true;
	PrimaryActorTick.bStartWithTickEnabled = false;

	Figure = CreateDefaultSubobject<UStaticMeshComponent>(TEXT("Figure"));
	Figure->SetupAttachment(Root);
	Figure->SetCollisionEnabled(ECollisionEnabled::NoCollision);
	Figure->SetGenerateOverlapEvents(false);
	Figure->SetCastShadow(false);
}

void AGBSpell_Trap::StartSpell()
{
	TriggerRadius = FMath::Max(1.f, ReadParam(GBSpellParam::TriggerRadius, 150.f));
	TriggerHeight = FMath::Max(1.f, SpellCast.Get(GBStat::Height, 200.f));
	const float ArmTime = ReadParam(GBSpellParam::ArmTime, 0.5f);
	const float Duration = ReadParam(GBStat::Duration, 20.f);

	SetActorLocationAndRotation(FindAimedGroundPoint(ReadParam(GBStat::Range, 1500.f)), FRotator(0.f, SpellCast.Direction.Rotation().Yaw, 0.f));

	const FString ColorStr = SpellCast.GetString(GBString::Color);
	FigureTint = ColorStr.IsEmpty() ? FLinearColor::White : UGBDataSubsystem::ParseColor(ColorStr, FLinearColor::White);
	FigureGlowValue = SpellCast.Get(GBSpellParam::FigureGlow, 1.f);

	const FName SpriteId = GetSpriteId();
	if (SpriteId.IsNone())
	{
		GBPlaceholder::SetupShape(Figure, EGBShape::Cylinder, FVector(TriggerRadius * 2.f, TriggerRadius * 2.f, TrapDiscThicknessCm),
			GetSpellColor() * TrapUnarmedBrightness, true);
		PlaceGroundFigure(Figure, TriggerRadius);
		Figure->AddWorldOffset(FVector(0.f, 0.f, TrapDiscThicknessCm * 0.5f));
		GroundFigureZ = Figure->GetComponentLocation().Z;
	}
	else
	{
		FLinearColor Dimmed = FigureTint * TrapUnarmedBrightness;
		Dimmed.A = 1.f;
		GBPlaceholder::SetupFlatSprite(Figure, SpriteId, TriggerRadius * 2.f, Dimmed, FigureGlowValue * TrapUnarmedBrightness);
		PlaceGroundFigure(Figure, TriggerRadius);
	}

	if (ArmTime > 0.f)
	{
		GetWorldTimerManager().SetTimer(ArmTimer, this, &AGBSpell_Trap::Arm, ArmTime, false);
	}
	else
	{
		Arm();
	}
	EndSpellAfter(Duration);
}

void AGBSpell_Trap::Arm()
{
	bArmed = true;
	if (GetSpriteId().IsNone())
	{
		GBPlaceholder::SetShapeColor(Figure, GetSpellColor());
	}
	else
	{
		GBPlaceholder::SetFlatSpriteLook(Figure, FigureTint, FigureGlowValue);
	}
	SetActorTickEnabled(true);
}

void AGBSpell_Trap::Tick(float DeltaSeconds)
{
	Super::Tick(DeltaSeconds);
	if (!bArmed || bEnded)
	{
		return;
	}
	TArray<AActor*> Targets;
	GatherHostilesOnDisc(GetActorLocation(), TriggerRadius, TriggerHeight, Targets);
	if (Targets.Num() > 0)
	{
		Trigger();
	}
}

void AGBSpell_Trap::Trigger()
{
	bArmed = false;
	SetActorTickEnabled(false);

	const FVector BlastCenter = GetActorLocation() + FVector::UpVector * SpellCast.Get(GBSpellParam::BlastHeight, 50.f);
	FGBDamageInfo Info = SpellCast.MakeDamageInfo(GBStat::Damage, this);
	Info.HitLocation = BlastCenter;
	const bool bFalloff = SpellCast.Get(GBSpellParam::Falloff, 0.f) > 0.5f;
	TArray<AActor*> Ignore;
	Ignore.Add(this);
	UGBCombatLibrary::DealRadialDamage(this, BlastCenter, FMath::Max(1.f, ReadParam(GBStat::Radius, 300.f)), Info, Ignore, bFalloff);
	EndSpell();
}
#include "Spells/GBSpell_Orb.h"
#include "Components/BillboardComponent.h"
#include "Components/StaticMeshComponent.h"
#include "Core/GBPlaceholder.h"
#include "Core/GBStatNames.h"
#include "Engine/World.h"
#include "Player/GBPlayerCharacter.h"
#include "TimerManager.h"

namespace
{
	/** Visual only: how bright the area sphere is compared with the spell color. */
	constexpr float OrbAreaBrightness = 0.15f;
}

AGBSpell_Orb::AGBSpell_Orb()
{
	PrimaryActorTick.bCanEverTick = true;
	PrimaryActorTick.bStartWithTickEnabled = false;

	Sprite = CreateDefaultSubobject<UBillboardComponent>(TEXT("Sprite"));
	Sprite->SetupAttachment(Root);

	Area = CreateDefaultSubobject<UStaticMeshComponent>(TEXT("Area"));
	Area->SetupAttachment(Root);
	Area->SetCollisionEnabled(ECollisionEnabled::NoCollision);
	Area->SetGenerateOverlapEvents(false);
	Area->SetCastShadow(false);
}

void AGBSpell_Orb::StartSpell()
{
	MoveSpeed = FMath::Max(0.f, ReadParam(GBStat::Speed, 400.f));
	RangeLeft = FMath::Max(0.f, ReadParam(GBStat::Range, 2000.f));
	OrbRadius = FMath::Max(1.f, ReadParam(GBStat::Radius, 250.f));
	const float Duration = ReadParam(GBStat::Duration, 6.f);
	const float Interval = SafeInterval(ReadParam(GBStat::TickInterval, 0.5f));
	DamagePerTick = FMath::Max(0.f, ReadParam(GBStat::DamagePerSecond, 20.f)) * Interval;

	MoveDirection = SpellCast.Direction.GetSafeNormal();
	if (MoveDirection.IsNearlyZero())
	{
		MoveDirection = GetActorForwardVector();
	}
	FVector Start = SpellCast.Origin;
	if (const AGBPlayerCharacter* Player = Cast<AGBPlayerCharacter>(GetCaster()))
	{
		Start = Player->GetMuzzleLocation();
	}
	SetActorLocation(Start);

	SetupSpellSprite(Sprite, 60.f);
	if (SpellCast.Get(GBSpellParam::ShowArea, 1.f) > 0.5f)
	{
		GBPlaceholder::SetupShape(Area, EGBShape::Sphere, FVector(OrbRadius * 2.f), GetSpellColor() * OrbAreaBrightness, true);
	}
	else
	{
		Area->SetVisibility(false);
	}

	bMoving = MoveSpeed > 0.f && RangeLeft > 0.f;
	SetActorTickEnabled(bMoving);
	GetWorldTimerManager().SetTimer(TickTimer, this, &AGBSpell_Orb::DamageTick, Interval, true, 0.f);
	EndSpellAfter(Duration);
}

void AGBSpell_Orb::Tick(float DeltaSeconds)
{
	Super::Tick(DeltaSeconds);
	if (!bMoving || bEnded)
	{
		return;
	}

	const float Step = FMath::Min(MoveSpeed * DeltaSeconds, RangeLeft);
	const FVector From = GetActorLocation();
	const FVector To = From + MoveDirection * Step;

	FCollisionQueryParams Params(SCENE_QUERY_STAT(GBSpellOrbMove), false, this);
	if (AActor* Caster = GetCaster())
	{
		Params.AddIgnoredActor(Caster);
	}
	FHitResult Hit;
	if (GetWorld()->LineTraceSingleByObjectType(Hit, From, To, FCollisionObjectQueryParams(ECC_WorldStatic), Params))
	{
		// Stops at the wall and keeps crackling there until its time runs out.
		SetActorLocation(Hit.Location);
		bMoving = false;
		SetActorTickEnabled(false);
		return;
	}

	SetActorLocation(To);
	RangeLeft -= Step;
	if (RangeLeft <= KINDA_SMALL_NUMBER)
	{
		EndSpell();
	}
}

void AGBSpell_Orb::DamageTick()
{
	if (DamagePerTick <= 0.f)
	{
		return;
	}
	TArray<AActor*> Targets;
	GatherHostilesInSphere(GetActorLocation(), OrbRadius, Targets);
	for (AActor* Target : Targets)
	{
		DamageTarget(Target, DamagePerTick, GetActorLocation());
	}
}
	const FRotator WallRotation(0.f, SpellCast.Direction.Rotation().Yaw, 0.f);
	const FVector Point = CasterLocation + WallRotation.Vector() * Distance;
	FVector Ground;
	if (!FindGround(Point + FVector::UpVector * Height, Ground, Height + WallGroundSearchExtraCm))
	{
		Ground = Point;
		Ground.Z = CasterLocation.Z - (Caster ? Caster->GetSimpleCollisionHalfHeight() : 0.f);
	}
	SetActorLocationAndRotation(Ground + FVector::UpVector * (Height * 0.5f), WallRotation, false, nullptr, ETeleportType::TeleportPhysics);

	const FVector Size(Thickness, Width, Height);
	Box->SetBoxExtent(Size * 0.5f);

	const FString TextureId = SpellCast.GetString(GBSpellString::WallTexture);
	if (!TextureId.IsEmpty())
	{
		// A structure (ice wall): one solid pixel-textured box, no camera-facing sprites (GDD 3).
		const float TileSize = ReadParam(GBSpellParam::WallTileSize, 100.f);
		const bool bGlow = SpellCast.Get(GBSpellParam::WallGlow, 0.f) > 0.5f;
		const FString TintStr = SpellCast.GetString(FName(TEXT("Tint")));
		const FLinearColor Tinted = TintStr.IsEmpty() ? FLinearColor::White : UGBDataSubsystem::ParseColor(TintStr, FLinearColor::White);
		GBPlaceholder::SetupTexturedShape(Visual, EGBShape::Cube, Size, FName(*TextureId), TileSize, Tinted, bGlow);
		Visual->SetCastShadow(true);
	}
	else
	{
		// An effect (fire wall): a glowing box plus a row of camera-facing sprites (pixel-art flames).
		GBPlaceholder::SetupShape(Visual, EGBShape::Cube, Size, GetSpellColor(), true);
	}

	if (TextureId.IsEmpty() && !GetSpriteId().IsNone())
	{
		const float SpriteHeight = FMath::Max(1.f, SpellCast.Get(GBSpellParam::SpriteSize, Height));
		const int32 Count = FMath::Clamp(FMath::RoundToInt(Width / SpriteHeight), 1, MaxWallSprites);
		for (int32 Index = 0; Index < Count; ++Index)
		{
			UBillboardComponent* Sprite = NewObject<UBillboardComponent>(this);
			Sprite->SetupAttachment(Root);
			Sprite->RegisterComponent();
			AddInstanceComponent(Sprite);
			Sprite->SetRelativeLocation(FVector(0.f, -Width * 0.5f + (Index + 0.5f) * Width / Count, 0.f));
			SetupSpellSprite(Sprite, Height);
			Sprites.Add(Sprite);
		}
	}

	if (bBlocking)
	{
		Box->SetCollisionProfileName(UCollisionProfile::BlockAllDynamic_ProfileName);
		// Projectiles only overlap WorldDynamic: without this they pass through the wall untouched.
		Box->SetGenerateOverlapEvents(true);
		SetupHealth(ReadParam(GBStat::Health, 100.f));
	}

	const float DamagePerSecond = SpellCast.Get(GBStat::DamagePerSecond, 0.f);
	if (DamagePerSecond > 0.f)
	{
		const float Interval = SafeInterval(ReadParam(GBStat::TickInterval, 0.5f));
		DamagePerTick = DamagePerSecond * Interval;
		GetWorldTimerManager().SetTimer(TickTimer, this, &AGBSpell_Wall::DamageTick, Interval, true);
	}

	EndSpellAfter(Duration);
}

void AGBSpell_Wall::SetupHealth(float Health)
{
	UGBStatsComponent* WallStats = NewObject<UGBStatsComponent>(this, TEXT("WallStats"));
	WallStats->RegisterComponent();
	AddInstanceComponent(WallStats);
	WallStats->SetBaseStat(GBStat::MaxHealth, FMath::Max(1.f, Health));
	WallStats->SetBaseStat(GBStat::DamageTaken, SpellCast.Get(GBStat::DamageTaken, 1.f));
	WallStats->SetBaseStat(GBStat::HealthRegen, SpellCast.Get(GBStat::HealthRegen, 0.f));
	WallStats->SetBaseStat(GBStat::MaxMana, 0.f);

	UGBAttributeComponent* WallAttributes = NewObject<UGBAttributeComponent>(this, TEXT("WallAttributes"));
	// Neutral: nobody targets the wall on purpose, but shots and enemies that hit it wear it down.
	WallAttributes->Team = EGBTeam::Neutral;
	WallAttributes->RegisterComponent();
	AddInstanceComponent(WallAttributes);
	WallAttributes->InitializeFromStats();
	WallAttributes->OnDeath.AddUObject(this, &AGBSpell_Wall::HandleWallDeath);
}

void AGBSpell_Wall::HandleWallDeath(const FGBDamageInfo& KillingBlow)
{
	// Not inside the damage call that killed it.
	EndSpellAfter(0.f);
}

void AGBSpell_Wall::DamageTick()
{
	TArray<AActor*> Targets;
	const FVector Extent = Box->GetUnscaledBoxExtent() + FVector(WallTouchMarginCm);
	GatherHostilesInBox(GetActorLocation(), GetActorQuat(), Extent, Targets);
	for (AActor* Target : Targets)
	{
		DamageTarget(Target, DamagePerTick, GetActorLocation());
	}
}

+	static void RunBuildUp(const TArray<FString>& Args, UWorld* World)
+	{
+		if (Args.Num() < 2)
+		{
+			UE_LOG(LogGB, Warning, TEXT("gb.BuildUp <Fire|Lightning|Ice> <Amount>"));
+			return;
+		}
+		EGBElement Element = EGBElement::Fire;
+		if (Args[0].Equals(TEXT("Fire"), ESearchCase::IgnoreCase))
+		{
+			Element = EGBElement::Fire;
+		}
+		else if (Args[0].Equals(TEXT("Lightning"), ESearchCase::IgnoreCase))
+		{
+			Element = EGBElement::Lightning;
+		}
+		else if (Args[0].Equals(TEXT("Ice"), ESearchCase::IgnoreCase))
+		{
+			Element = EGBElement::Ice;
+		}
+		else
+		{
+			UE_LOG(LogGB, Warning, TEXT("gb.BuildUp: unknown element '%s' (Fire|Lightning|Ice)"), *Args[0]);
+			return;
+		}
+		const float Amount = FCString::Atof(*Args[1]);
+		AGBPlayerCharacter* Player = AGBPlayerCharacter::Get(World);
+		if (!Player)
+		{
+			UE_LOG(LogGB, Warning, TEXT("gb.BuildUp: no player character"));
+			return;
+		}
+		const FVector PlayerLoc = Player->GetActorLocation();
+		for (TActorIterator<AGBEnemyCharacter> It(World); It; ++It)
+		{
+			AGBEnemyCharacter* Enemy = *It;
+			if (!Enemy || Enemy->IsDead())
+			{
+				continue;
+			}
+			if (FVector::Dist(PlayerLoc, Enemy->GetActorLocation()) > 1500.f)
+			{
+				continue;
+			}
+			FGBDamageInfo Info;
+			Info.Element = Element;
+			Info.Instigator = Player;
+			Info.BuildUp = Amount;
+			Info.BuildUpEventId = UGBCombatLibrary::NewBuildUpEventId();
+			UGBCombatLibrary::ApplyBuildUp(Enemy, Info);
+			if (const UGBElementalStatusComponent* Elemental = Enemy->GetElementalStatus())
+			{
+				UE_LOG(LogGB, Display, TEXT("BuildUp %s: %.1f/%.1f phase=%d"),
+					*Enemy->GetEnemyId().ToString(),
+					Elemental->GetBuildUp(Element), Elemental->GetThreshold(Element),
+					static_cast<int32>(Elemental->GetPhase(Element)));
+			}
+		}
+	}

bool AGBHeldItem::IsAutomatic() const
{
	const FGBWeaponDefinition* Def = GetDefinition();
	return Def && Def->bAutomatic;
}

UGBStatsComponent* AGBHeldItem::GetOwnerStats() const
{
	const AGBPlayerCharacter* Player = GetPlayerOwner();
	return Player ? Player->GetStats() : nullptr;
}

bool AGBHeldItem::GetActiveEnchant(EGBElement& OutElement, FName& OutOnHitEffect) const
{
	const AGBPlayerCharacter* Player = GetPlayerOwner();
	const UGBStatusEffectComponent* Effects = Player ? Player->GetStatusEffects() : nullptr;
	const UGBDataSubsystem* Data = UGBDataSubsystem::Get(this);
	if (!Effects || !Data)
	{
		return false;
	}

	for (const FGBActiveStatusEffect& Active : Effects->GetActiveEffects())
	{
		const FGBStatusEffectDefinition* Def = Data->FindStatusEffect(Active.EffectId);
		if (!Def)
		{
			continue;
		}
		const FString* ElementName = Def->Strings.Find(GBWeaponString::WeaponElement);
		if (!ElementName || ElementName->IsEmpty())
		{
			continue;
		}
		const int64 Value = StaticEnum<EGBElement>()->GetValueByNameString(*ElementName);
		if (Value == INDEX_NONE)
		{
			UE_LOG(LogGB, Warning, TEXT("Status effect '%s': unknown WeaponElement '%s'"), *Active.EffectId.ToString(), **ElementName);
			continue;
		}
		OutElement = static_cast<EGBElement>(Value);
		const FString* Effect = Def->Strings.Find(GBWeaponString::WeaponOnHitEffect);
		OutOnHitEffect = (Effect && !Effect->IsEmpty()) ? FName(**Effect) : NAME_None;
		return true;
	}
	return false;
}

FGBDamageInfo AGBHeldItem::MakeDamageInfo() const
{
	FGBDamageInfo Info;
	Info.Amount = GetStat(GBStat::Damage);
	Info.Element = Element;
	Info.Instigator = GetPlayerOwner();
	Info.Causer = const_cast<AGBHeldItem*>(this);
	Info.SourceId = WeaponId;

	if (const FGBWeaponDefinition* Def = GetDefinition())
	{
		if (const FString* Effect = Def->Strings.Find(GBString::OnHitEffect))
		{
			if (!Effect->IsEmpty())
			{
				Info.OnHitEffect = FName(**Effect);
			}
		}
	}

	// The enchant's damage multiplier arrives by itself through its "Weapon.Gun.Damage" modifier.
	EGBElement EnchantElement = EGBElement::Physical;
	FName EnchantEffect = NAME_None;
	if (AcceptsEnchant() && GetActiveEnchant(EnchantElement, EnchantEffect))
	{
		Info.Element = EnchantElement;
		if (!EnchantEffect.IsNone())
		{
			Info.OnHitEffect = EnchantEffect;
		}
	}

	const int32 ElementIndex = GBElemental::ElementIndex(Info.Element);
	if (ElementIndex >= 0)
	{
		Info.BuildUp = GetStat(GBElemental::WeaponBuildUpKey(ElementIndex), 0.f);
		const UGBDataSubsystem* Data = UGBDataSubsystem::Get(this);
		const float DefaultMult = Data ? Data->GetGameValue(GBStat::WeakPointBuildUpMult, 1.f) : 1.f;
		Info.WeakPointBuildUpMult = GetStat(GBStat::WeakPointBuildUpMult, DefaultMult);
	}
	Info.BuildUpEventId = UGBCombatLibrary::NewBuildUpEventId();
	return Info;
}

int32 AGBGun::GetMagazineSize() const
{
	return FMath::Max(0, FMath::RoundToInt(GetStat(GBStat::MagazineSize, 1.f)));
}

int32 AGBGun::GetMaxSpareMagazines() const
{
	return FMath::Max(0, FMath::RoundToInt(GetStat(GBStat::SpareMagazines, 0.f)));
}

void AGBGun::SetAmmo(const FGBAmmoState& InAmmo)
{
	CancelReload();
	Ammo.InMagazine = FMath::Clamp(InAmmo.InMagazine, 0, GetMagazineSize());
	Ammo.SpareMagazines = FMath::Clamp(InAmmo.SpareMagazines, 0, GetMaxSpareMagazines());
	NotifyEquipmentChanged();
}

void AGBGun::RefillAmmo()
{
	CancelReload();
	Ammo.InMagazine = GetMagazineSize();
	Ammo.SpareMagazines = GetMaxSpareMagazines();
	NotifyEquipmentChanged();
}

int32 AGBGun::AddSpareMagazines(int32 Count)
{
	if (Count <= 0)
	{
		return 0;
	}
	const int32 Added = FMath::Clamp(GetMaxSpareMagazines() - Ammo.SpareMagazines, 0, Count);
	if (Added > 0)
	{
		Ammo.SpareMagazines += Added;
		NotifyEquipmentChanged();
	}
	return Added;
}

// ---- Reload ----

void AGBGun::Reload()
{
	if (bReloading || !bEquipped)
	{
		return;
	}
	if (Ammo.InMagazine >= GetMagazineSize() || Ammo.SpareMagazines <= 0)
	{
		return;
	}
	bReloading = true;
	ReloadElapsed = 0.f;
	ReloadDuration = FMath::Max(0.f, GetStat(GBStat::ReloadTime, 1.f));
	NotifyEquipmentChanged();
}

void AGBGun::CancelReload()
{
	if (!bReloading)
	{
		return;
	}
	bReloading = false;
	ReloadElapsed = 0.f;
	NotifyEquipmentChanged();
}

void AGBGun::FinishReload()
{
	bReloading = false;
	ReloadElapsed = 0.f;
	if (Ammo.SpareMagazines > 0)
	{
		// The rounds left in the old magazine are lost (provisional, classic tactical style).
		--Ammo.SpareMagazines;
		Ammo.InMagazine = GetMagazineSize();
	}
	NotifyEquipmentChanged();
}

float AGBGun::GetReloadProgress() const
{
	if (!bReloading)
	{
		return 0.f;
	}
	return ReloadDuration > KINDA_SMALL_NUMBER ? FMath::Clamp(ReloadElapsed / ReloadDuration, 0.f, 1.f) : 1.f;
}

// ---- Aim ----

void AGBGun::StartSecondary()
{
	if (bEquipped)
	{
		SetAiming(true);
	}
}

void AGBGun::StopSecondary()
{
	SetAiming(false);
}

void AGBGun::SetAiming(bool bInAiming)
{
	if (bAiming == bInAiming)
	{
		return;
	}
	bAiming = bInAiming;
	if (AGBPlayerCharacter* Player = GetPlayerOwner())
	{
		Player->SetMoveSpeedScale(bAiming ? GetStat(GBStat::AimMoveSpeedMult, 1.f) : 1.f);
	}
}

void AGBGun::UpdateAimFOV(float DeltaSeconds, bool bInstant)
{
	const AGBPlayerCharacter* Player = GetPlayerOwner();
	UCameraComponent* Camera = Player ? Player->GetCamera() : nullptr;
	if (!Camera)
	{
		return;
	}

	const float Target = bAiming ? 1.f : 0.f;
	if (AimAlpha == Target)
	{
		return;
	}
	if (AimAlpha <= 0.f)
	{
		HipFOV = Camera->FieldOfView;
	}

	if (bInstant)
	{
		AimAlpha = Target;
	}
	else
	{
		const float AimTime = GetStat(GBWeaponStat::AimTime, 0.15f);
		const float Step = AimTime > KINDA_SMALL_NUMBER ? DeltaSeconds / AimTime : 1.f;
		AimAlpha = FMath::Clamp(Target > AimAlpha ? AimAlpha + Step : AimAlpha - Step, 0.f, 1.f);
	}
	Camera->SetFieldOfView(FMath::Lerp(HipFOV, GetStat(GBStat::AimFOV, HipFOV), AimAlpha));
}

void AGBGun::OnUnequipped()
{
	CancelReload();
	SetAiming(false);
	UpdateAimFOV(0.f, true);
	Super::OnUnequipped();
}

void AGBGun::Tick(float DeltaSeconds)
{
	if (bReloading)
	{
		ReloadElapsed += DeltaSeconds;
		if (ReloadElapsed >= ReloadDuration)
		{
			FinishReload();
		}
	}
	if (bEquipped)
	{
		UpdateAimFOV(DeltaSeconds, false);
	}
	Super::Tick(DeltaSeconds);
}

bool AGBGun::PerformAttack()
{
	AGBPlayerCharacter* Player = GetPlayerOwner();
	if (!Player || !GetWorld() || bReloading)
	{
		return false;
#include "Weapons/GBSword.h"

#include "Combat/GBCombatLibrary.h"
#include "Components/CapsuleComponent.h"

namespace
{
	UStaticMeshComponent* MakeSwordHiltPart(AActor* Owner, USceneComponent* Parent, FName Name, EGBShape Shape, const FVector& Location,
		const FVector& Size, const FLinearColor& Color, bool bGlow)
	{
		UStaticMeshComponent* Part = NewObject<UStaticMeshComponent>(Owner, Name);
		Part->SetupAttachment(Parent);
		Part->SetRelativeLocation(Location);
		Part->SetCollisionEnabled(ECollisionEnabled::NoCollision);
		Part->SetGenerateOverlapEvents(false);
		Part->SetCastShadow(false);
		Part->RegisterComponent();
		GBPlaceholder::SetupShape(Part, Shape, Size, Color, bGlow);
		return Part;
	}
}

void AGBSword::InitFromDefinition(const FGBWeaponDefinition& Definition, AGBPlayerCharacter* InOwner)
{
	Super::InitFromDefinition(Definition, InOwner);

	const FName Required[] = { GBStat::Damage, GBStat::FireInterval, GBStat::MeleeRange, GBStat::MeleeArc };
	for (const FName& Stat : Required)
	{
		if (!Definition.Stats.Contains(Stat))
		{
			UE_LOG(LogGB, Warning, TEXT("Weapons.json '%s': missing stat '%s', using a default"), *Definition.Id.ToString(), *Stat.ToString());
		}
	}

	// Placeholder 3D hilt (art rule: the hilt comes from the future). Sizes in cm are purely visual; the blade
	// rises along +Z from the guard and is drawn by the HUD as pixel art.
	Grip = MakeSwordHiltPart(this, Root, TEXT("Grip"), EGBShape::Cylinder, FVector(0.f, 0.f, -5.f), FVector(3.f, 3.f, 10.f), FLinearColor(0.10f, 0.10f, 0.12f), false);
	Guard = MakeSwordHiltPart(this, Root, TEXT("Guard"), EGBShape::Cube, FVector(0.f, 0.f, 1.f), FVector(4.f, 14.f, 2.f), FLinearColor(0.35f, 0.35f, 0.40f), false);
	ShownElement = Element;
	Gem = MakeSwordHiltPart(this, Root, TEXT("Gem"), EGBShape::Sphere, FVector(2.5f, 0.f, 1.f), FVector(2.5f, 2.5f, 2.5f),
		GBPlaceholder::ElementColor(static_cast<uint8>(ShownElement)), true);
}

void AGBSword::Tick(float DeltaSeconds)
{
	if (Gem && ShownElement != Element)
	{
		ShownElement = Element;
		GBPlaceholder::SetShapeColor(Gem, GBPlaceholder::ElementColor(static_cast<uint8>(ShownElement)));
	}
	Super::Tick(DeltaSeconds);
}

void AGBSword::GetBodySegment(AActor* Target, FVector& OutBottom, FVector& OutTop)
{
	if (const ACharacter* Character = Cast<ACharacter>(Target))
	{
		if (const UCapsuleComponent* Capsule = Character->GetCapsuleComponent())
		{
			const float HalfHeight = Capsule->GetScaledCapsuleHalfHeight();
			const FVector Center = Character->GetActorLocation();
			OutBottom = FVector(Center.X, Center.Y, Center.Z - HalfHeight);
			OutTop = FVector(Center.X, Center.Y, Center.Z + HalfHeight);
			return;
		}
	}
	if (const UCapsuleComponent* Capsule = Target ? Target->FindComponentByClass<UCapsuleComponent>() : nullptr)
	{
		const float HalfHeight = Capsule->GetScaledCapsuleHalfHeight();
		const FVector Center = Capsule->GetComponentLocation();
		OutBottom = FVector(Center.X, Center.Y, Center.Z - HalfHeight);
		OutTop = FVector(Center.X, Center.Y, Center.Z + HalfHeight);
		return;
	}
	FVector Origin;
	FVector Extent;
	Target->GetActorBounds(false, Origin, Extent, false);
	OutBottom = FVector(Origin.X, Origin.Y, Origin.Z - Extent.Z);
	OutTop = FVector(Origin.X, Origin.Y, Origin.Z + Extent.Z);
}

bool AGBSword::PerformAttack()
{
	AGBPlayerCharacter* Player = GetPlayerOwner();
	UWorld* World = GetWorld();
	if (!Player || !World)
	{
		return false;
	}

	const FVector Origin = Player->GetAimOrigin();
	const FVector Forward = Player->GetAimDirection().GetSafeNormal();
	const float Range = GetStat(GBStat::MeleeRange, 200.f);
	const float HalfArcCos = FMath::Cos(FMath::DegreesToRadians(FMath::Clamp(GetStat(GBStat::MeleeArc, 90.f), 0.f, 360.f) * 0.5f));
	if (Range <= 0.f)
	{
		return true;
	}

	FCollisionObjectQueryParams ObjectParams;
	ObjectParams.AddObjectTypesToQuery(ECC_Pawn);
	ObjectParams.AddObjectTypesToQuery(ECC_WorldDynamic);
	FCollisionQueryParams OverlapParams(SCENE_QUERY_STAT(GBSwordOverlap), false, Player);
	OverlapParams.AddIgnoredActor(this);

	TArray<FOverlapResult> Overlaps;
	World->OverlapMultiByObjectType(Overlaps, Origin, FQuat::Identity, ObjectParams, FCollisionShape::MakeSphere(Range), OverlapParams);

	const FGBDamageInfo BaseInfo = MakeDamageInfo();
	const float HeadshotMult = GetStat(GBStat::HeadshotMult, 1.f);
	TSet<AActor*> Done;
	for (const FOverlapResult& Overlap : Overlaps)
	{
		AActor* Target = Overlap.GetActor();
		if (!Target || Done.Contains(Target))
		{
			continue;
		}
		Done.Add(Target);

		if (!UGBAttributeComponent::Find(Target) || !UGBCombatLibrary::IsAlive(Target) || !UGBCombatLibrary::AreHostile(Player, Target))
		{
			continue;
		}

		// Arc and range selection stays on the target's center, so nothing previously hit is missed.
		const FVector TargetLocation = Target->GetActorLocation();
		const FVector ToTarget = TargetLocation - Origin;
		const FVector ToTargetDir = ToTarget.GetSafeNormal();
		if (!ToTargetDir.IsNearlyZero() && FVector::DotProduct(Forward, ToTargetDir) < HalfArcCos)
		{
			continue;
		}

		FVector BodyBottom;
		FVector BodyTop;
		GetBodySegment(Target, BodyBottom, BodyTop);
		const FVector HitLocation = UGBCombatLibrary::ClosestBodyPointToAim(Origin, Forward, Range, BodyBottom, BodyTop);

		// Not through walls: trace to the aimed hit location, not the target's center.
		FCollisionQueryParams WallParams(SCENE_QUERY_STAT(GBSwordWall), false, Player);
		WallParams.AddIgnoredActor(this);
		WallParams.AddIgnoredActor(Target);
		if (World->LineTraceTestByChannel(Origin, HitLocation, ECC_Visibility, WallParams))
		{
			continue;
		}

		FGBDamageInfo Info = BaseInfo;
		Info.HitLocation = HitLocation;
		Info.HitDirection = ToTargetDir;
		UGBCombatLibrary::ApplyWeakPointBonus(Target, Info, HeadshotMult);
		UGBCombatLibrary::DealDamage(Target, Info);
	}
	return true;
}

void FTickFunction::SetTickFunctionEnable(bool bInEnabled)
{
	if (IsTickFunctionRegistered())
	{
		if (bInEnabled == (TickState == ETickState::Disabled))
		{
			FTickTaskLevel* TickTaskLevel = InternalData->TickTaskLevel;
			check(TickTaskLevel);
			TickTaskLevel->RemoveTickFunction(this);
			TickState = (bInEnabled ? ETickState::Enabled : ETickState::Disabled);
			TickTaskLevel->AddTickFunction(this);
		}

		if (TickState == ETickState::Disabled)
		{
			InternalData->LastIntervalTickSeconds = -1.0;
		}
	}
	else
	{
		TickState = (bInEnabled ? ETickState::Enabled : ETickState::Disabled);
	}
}

void FTickFunction::UpdateTickIntervalAndCoolDown(float NewTickInterval)
{
	TickInterval = NewTickInterval;
	if(IsTickFunctionRegistered() && TickState != ETickState::Disabled && InternalData->bWasInterval)
	{
		FTickTaskLevel* TickTaskLevel = InternalData->TickTaskLevel;
		check(TickTaskLevel);

		// Try to update the interval from the reschedule list
		if (!TickTaskLevel->UpdateRescheduleInterval(this, TickInterval))
		{
			// If is was not in the reschedule list means it needs to be removed from the cooldown list and rescheduled.
			TickTaskLevel->RemoveTickFunction(this);
			TickTaskLevel->RescheduleForInterval(this, TickInterval);
		}
	}
}

void FTickFunction::AddPrerequisite(UObject* TargetObject, struct FTickFunction& TargetTickFunction)
{
	const bool bThisCanTick = (bCanEverTick || IsTickFunctionRegistered());
	const bool bTargetCanTick = (TargetTickFunction.bCanEverTick || TargetTickFunction.IsTickFunctionRegistered());

	if (bThisCanTick && bTargetCanTick)
	{
		Prerequisites.AddUnique(FTickPrerequisite(TargetObject, TargetTickFunction));
	}
float FTickFunction::CalculateDeltaTime(float DeltaTime, const UWorld* TickingWorld)
{
	if (!InternalData->bWasInterval)
	{
		// No tick interval. Return the world delta seconds, and make sure to mark that
		// we're not tracking last-tick-time for this object.
		InternalData->LastIntervalTickSeconds = -1.0;
	}
	else
	{
		// We've got a tick interval. Mark last-tick-time. If we already had last-tick-time, return
		// the time since then; otherwise, return the world delta seconds.
		const double CurrentWorldTime = (bTickEvenWhenPaused ? TickingWorld->GetUnpausedTimeSeconds() : TickingWorld->GetTimeSeconds());
		if (InternalData->LastIntervalTickSeconds >= 0.0)
		{
			DeltaTime = CurrentWorldTime - InternalData->LastIntervalTickSeconds;
		}
		InternalData->LastIntervalTickSeconds = CurrentWorldTime;
	}

	return DeltaTime;
}

/**
 * Singleton to retrieve the global tick task manager
 * @return Reference to the global tick task manager
**/
FTickTaskManagerInterface& FTickTaskManagerInterface::Get()
{
	return FTickTaskManager::Get();
}

float FGBSpellCast::Get(FName Param, float DefaultValue) const
{
	const float* Value = Values.Find(Param);
	return Value ? *Value : DefaultValue;
}

bool FGBSpellCast::Has(FName Param) const
{
	return Values.Contains(Param);
}

FString FGBSpellCast::GetString(FName Key, const FString& DefaultValue) const
{
	const FString* Value = Strings.Find(Key);
	return Value ? *Value : DefaultValue;
}

FGBDamageInfo FGBSpellCast::MakeDamageInfo(FName AmountParam, AActor* Causer) const
{
	FGBDamageInfo Info;
	Info.Amount = Get(AmountParam);
	Info.Element = Element;
	Info.Instigator = Caster;
	Info.Causer = Causer;
	Info.SourceId = SpellId;
	if (const FString* Effect = Strings.Find(GBString::OnHitEffect))
	{
		if (!Effect->IsEmpty())
		{
			Info.OnHitEffect = FName(**Effect);
		}
	}
	Info.EffectDurationScale = Get(GBStat::EffectDuration, 1.f);
	Info.EffectStrengthScale = Get(GBStat::EffectStrength, 1.f);
	Info.BuildUp = Get(GBStat::BuildUp, 0.f);
	Info.BuildUpEventId = UGBCombatLibrary::NewBuildUpEventId();
	return Info;
}

namespace GBSpell
{
	static float ApplySpellModifiers(FName SpellId, FName Param, float Value, const UGBStatsComponent* Stats)
	{
		if (!Stats)
		{
			return Value;
		}
		const FName Keys[] = { GBStat::SpellKey(SpellId, Param), GBStat::SpellAllKey(Param) };
		return Stats->EvaluateKeys(Keys, Value);
	}

	/** Upper limit applied after QTE scaling AND modifiers. Cap <= 0 = no cap. */
	static float ApplyParamCap(const FGBScaledParam& Scaled, float Value)
	{
		return Scaled.Cap > 0.f ? FMath::Min(Value, Scaled.Cap) : Value;
	}

	FGBSpellCast ResolveCast(const FGBSpellDefinition& Definition, float Power, float Reach, bool bUseQTE, const UGBStatsComponent* Stats)
	{
		FGBSpellCast Result;
		Result.SpellId = Definition.Id;
		Result.Element = Definition.Element;
		Result.Power = Power;
		Result.Reach = Reach;
		Result.bUsedQTE = bUseQTE;
		Result.Strings = Definition.Strings;
		Result.Values.Reserve(Definition.Params.Num());
		for (const TPair<FName, FGBScaledParam>& Pair : Definition.Params)
		{
			const float Value = Pair.Value.Resolve(Power, Reach, bUseQTE);
			Result.Values.Add(Pair.Key, ApplyParamCap(Pair.Value, ApplySpellModifiers(Definition.Id, Pair.Key, Value, Stats)));
		}
		return Result;
	}

	float ResolveParam(const FGBSpellDefinition& Definition, FName Param, float Power, float Reach, bool bUseQTE, const UGBStatsComponent* Stats, float DefaultValue)
	{
		const FGBScaledParam* Scaled = Definition.Params.Find(Param);
		if (!Scaled)
		{
			return DefaultValue;
		}
		return ApplyParamCap(*Scaled, ApplySpellModifiers(Definition.Id, Param, Scaled->Resolve(Power, Reach, bUseQTE), Stats));
	}
}
}

void AGBSpellActor::EndSpellAfter(float Seconds)
{
	if (bEnded)
	{
		return;
	}
	if (Seconds <= 0.f)
	{
		GetWorldTimerManager().SetTimerForNextTick(this, &AGBSpellActor::EndSpell);
	}
	else
	{
		GetWorldTimerManager().SetTimer(EndTimer, this, &AGBSpellActor::EndSpell, Seconds, false);
	}
}

void AGBSpellActor::DamageTarget(AActor* Target, float Amount, const FVector& SourceLocation)
{
	if (!Target)
	{
		return;
	}
	FGBDamageInfo Info = SpellCast.MakeDamageInfo(NAME_None, this);
	Info.Amount = Amount;
	Info.HitLocation = Target->GetActorLocation();
	Info.HitDirection = (Target->GetActorLocation() - SourceLocation).GetSafeNormal();
	UGBCombatLibrary::DealDamage(Target, Info);
}

void AGBSpellActor::ApplyOnHitEffect(AActor* Target) const
{
	const FString EffectId = SpellCast.GetString(GBString::OnHitEffect);
	if (!Target || EffectId.IsEmpty())
	{
		return;
	}
	if (UGBStatusEffectComponent* Effects = UGBStatusEffectComponent::Find(Target))
	{
		Effects->ApplyEffect(FName(*EffectId), GetCaster(),
			SpellCast.Get(GBStat::EffectDuration, 1.f), SpellCast.Get(GBStat::EffectStrength, 1.f));
	}
}

float AGBSpellActor::SafeInterval(float Interval)
{
	return FMath::Max(Interval, SpellActorMinTickInterval);
}

/** Elemental build-up keys (GDD 6): per-enemy stats, Game.json defaults, weapon stats, reactions. */
namespace GBElemental
{
	constexpr int32 NumElements = 3;

	inline int32 ElementIndex(EGBElement Element)
	{
		switch (Element)
		{
		case EGBElement::Fire: return 0;
		case EGBElement::Lightning: return 1;
		case EGBElement::Ice: return 2;
		default: return -1;
		}
	}

	inline EGBElement IndexToElement(int32 Index)
	{
		switch (Index)
		{
		case 0: return EGBElement::Fire;
		case 1: return EGBElement::Lightning;
		case 2: return EGBElement::Ice;
		default: return EGBElement::Physical;
		}
	}

	inline const FName& DamageTakenKey(int32 Index)
	{
		static const FName Keys[3] = { FName(TEXT("FireDamageTaken")), FName(TEXT("LightningDamageTaken")), FName(TEXT("IceDamageTaken")) };
		static const FName None = NAME_None;
		return (Index >= 0 && Index < 3) ? Keys[Index] : None;
	}

	inline const FName& BuildUpTakenKey(int32 Index)

	inline const FName ReactionNone(TEXT("None"));

	/** Default of enemy key Key in Game.json: "ElementalDefault.<Key>". Built once per key. */
	inline FName DefaultKey(FName Key)
	{
		static TMap<FName, FName> Cache;
		if (const FName* Found = Cache.Find(Key))
		{
			return *Found;
		}
		const FName Result(*FString(TEXT("ElementalDefault.")) + Key.ToString());
		Cache.Add(Key, Result);
		return Result;

	template <typename T>
	const T* FindIn(const TArray<T>& Rows, const TMap<FName, int32>& Index, FName Id)
	{
		const int32* Found = Index.Find(Id);
		return (Found && Rows.IsValidIndex(*Found)) ? &Rows[*Found] : nullptr;
	}
}

UGBDataSubsystem* UGBDataSubsystem::Get(const UObject* WorldContextObject)
{
	if (!WorldContextObject)
	{
		return nullptr;
	}
	if (const UGameInstance* DirectInstance = Cast<UGameInstance>(WorldContextObject))
	{
		return DirectInstance->GetSubsystem<UGBDataSubsystem>();
	}
	const UWorld* World = GEngine ? GEngine->GetWorldFromContextObject(WorldContextObject, EGetWorldErrorMode::ReturnNull) : nullptr;
	const UGameInstance* GameInstance = World ? World->GetGameInstance() : nullptr;
	return GameInstance ? GameInstance->GetSubsystem<UGBDataSubsystem>() : nullptr;
}

void UGBDataSubsystem::Initialize(FSubsystemCollectionBase& Collection)
{
	Super::Initialize(Collection);
	LoadAll();
}

void UGBDataSubsystem::ReloadAll()
{
	LoadAll();
	UE_LOG(LogGB, Log, TEXT("Data: reloaded from %s"), *GetDataDir());
	OnDataReloaded.Broadcast();
}

FString UGBDataSubsystem::GetDataDir()
{
	return FPaths::ProjectContentDir() / TEXT("Data/");
}

void UGBDataSubsystem::LoadAll()
{
	using namespace GBDataPrivate;

	LoadStruct(TEXT("Player.json"), Player);
	LoadStruct(TEXT("QTE.json"), QTE);
	LoadStruct(TEXT("Game.json"), Game);

	LoadArray(TEXT("Spells.json"), TEXT("Spells"), Spells);
	LoadArray(TEXT("Weapons.json"), TEXT("Weapons"), Weapons);
	LoadArray(TEXT("StatusEffects.json"), TEXT("StatusEffects"), StatusEffects);
#include "Spells/GBSpell_Projectile.h"
#include "Combat/GBProjectile.h"
#include "Core/GBStatNames.h"
#include "Engine/World.h"
#include "Player/GBPlayerCharacter.h"

namespace
{
	// Extra seconds of life on top of Range / Speed, so a projectile is never removed before reaching its range.
	constexpr float ProjectileSpellLifetimeMargin = 1.f;
}

void AGBSpell_Projectile::StartSpell()
{
	UWorld* World = GetWorld();
	if (!World)
	{
		EndSpell();
		return;
	}

	FGBProjectileConfig Config;
	Config.Damage = SpellCast.MakeDamageInfo(GBStat::Damage, this);
	Config.Speed = FMath::Max(1.f, ReadParam(GBStat::Speed, 3000.f));
	Config.Radius = FMath::Max(1.f, ReadParam(GBStat::Radius, 12.f));
	Config.MaxRange = FMath::Max(0.f, ReadParam(GBStat::Range, 5000.f));
	Config.ExplosionRadius = FMath::Max(0.f, SpellCast.Get(GBSpellParam::ExplosionRadius, 0.f));
	Config.bExplosionFalloff = SpellCast.Get(GBSpellParam::ExplosionFalloff, 1.f) > 0.5f;
	Config.ExplosionKnockback = FMath::Max(0.f, SpellCast.Get(GBSpellParam::ExplosionKnockback, Config.ExplosionKnockback));
	Config.ExplosionEffectTime = FMath::Max(0.f, SpellCast.Get(GBSpellParam::ExplosionEffectTime, Config.ExplosionEffectTime));
	Config.GravityScale = SpellCast.Get(GBStat::ProjectileGravity, 0.f);
	Config.bPierce = SpellCast.Get(GBSpellParam::Pierce, 0.f) > 0.5f;
	Config.HeadshotMult = SpellCast.Get(GBStat::HeadshotMult, 1.f);
	Config.bPixelArt = true;
	Config.SpriteId = GetSpriteId();
	Config.Color = GetSpellColor();
	Config.VisualScale = FMath::Max(0.01f, SpellCast.Get(GBSpellParam::VisualScale, 1.f));
	if (Config.MaxRange > 0.f)
	{
		Config.MaxLifetime = FMath::Max(Config.MaxLifetime, Config.MaxRange / Config.Speed + ProjectileSpellLifetimeMargin);
	}

	// Aim at what the crosshair is on, then fire from the hand so the shot does not start inside the camera.
	const FVector AimDir = SpellCast.Direction.GetSafeNormal().IsNearlyZero() ? GetActorForwardVector() : SpellCast.Direction.GetSafeNormal();
	const float TraceLength = Config.MaxRange > 0.f ? Config.MaxRange : Config.Speed * Config.MaxLifetime;
	FVector Target = SpellCast.Origin + AimDir * TraceLength;
	{
		FCollisionQueryParams Params(SCENE_QUERY_STAT(GBSpellProjectileAim), false, this);
		if (AActor* Caster = GetCaster())
		{
			Params.AddIgnoredActor(Caster);
		}
		FHitResult Hit;
		if (World->LineTraceSingleByChannel(Hit, SpellCast.Origin, Target, ECC_Visibility, Params))
		{
			Target = Hit.ImpactPoint;
		}
	}

	FVector Muzzle = SpellCast.Origin;
	if (const AGBPlayerCharacter* Player = Cast<AGBPlayerCharacter>(GetCaster()))
	{
		Muzzle = Player->GetMuzzleLocation();
	}
	FVector ShotDir = (Target - Muzzle).GetSafeNormal();
	if (ShotDir.IsNearlyZero() || FVector::DotProduct(ShotDir, AimDir) <= 0.f)
	{
		// The target is between the camera and the hand (point-blank wall): just fire along the aim.
		ShotDir = AimDir;
	}

	if (!AGBProjectile::SpawnProjectile(World, Config, Muzzle, ShotDir, GetCaster()))
	{
		UE_LOG(LogGB, Warning, TEXT("Spell '%s': the projectile could not be spawned."), *SpellCast.SpellId.ToString());
	}
	EndSpell();
}
#include "Weapons/GBStaff.h"

#include "Combat/GBProjectile.h"
#include "Core/GBPlaceholder.h"
#include "Core/GBStatNames.h"
#include "Player/GBPlayerCharacter.h"
#include "Stats/GBAttributeComponent.h"

bool AGBStaff::PerformAttack()
{
	AGBPlayerCharacter* Player = GetPlayerOwner();
	UWorld* World = GetWorld();
	if (!Player || !World)
	{
		return false;
	}

	const float ManaCost = GetStat(GBStat::ManaCost, 0.f);
	if (ManaCost > 0.f)
	{
		UGBAttributeComponent* Attributes = Player->GetAttributes();
		if (!Attributes || !Attributes->SpendMana(ManaCost))
		{
			return false;
		}
	}

	const float Range = GetStat(GBStat::Range, 5000.f);
	const FVector Muzzle = Player->GetMuzzleLocation();
	FVector Direction = FindAimPoint(Range) - Muzzle;
	if (Direction.IsNearlyZero())
	{
		Direction = Player->GetAimDirection();
	}

	FGBProjectileConfig Config;
	Config.Damage = MakeDamageInfo();
	Config.HeadshotMult = GetStat(GBStat::HeadshotMult, 1.f);
	Config.Speed = GetStat(GBStat::ProjectileSpeed, 3000.f);
	Config.Radius = GetStat(GBStat::ProjectileRadius, 10.f);
	Config.GravityScale = GetStat(GBStat::ProjectileGravity, 0.f);
	Config.MaxRange = Range;
	Config.bPixelArt = true;
	Config.Color = GBPlaceholder::ElementColor(static_cast<uint8>(Config.Damage.Element));
	if (const FGBWeaponDefinition* Def = GetDefinition())
	{
		if (const FString* Sprite = Def->Strings.Find(GBString::ProjectileSprite))
		{
			Config.SpriteId = FName(**Sprite);
		}
	}

	return AGBProjectile::SpawnProjectile(World, Config, Muzzle, Direction, Player) != nullptr;
}

	{
		Reload();
	}
	return true;

void AGBGun::FireHitscan(const FGBDamageInfo& BaseInfo, float SpreadDegrees)
{
	AGBPlayerCharacter* Player = GetPlayerOwner();
	UWorld* World = GetWorld();

	const FVector Origin = Player->GetAimOrigin();
	const FVector AimDirection = Player->GetAimDirection();
	const FVector Muzzle = Player->GetMuzzleLocation();
	const float Range = GetStat(GBStat::Range, 5000.f);
	const int32 Pellets = FMath::Max(1, FMath::RoundToInt(GetStat(GBStat::Pellets, 1.f)));
	const float FalloffStart = GetStat(GBStat::FalloffStart, 0.f);
	const float FalloffEnd = GetStat(GBStat::FalloffEnd, 0.f);
	const float NearMult = GetStat(GBStat::FalloffNearMult, 1.f);
	const float FarMult = GetStat(GBStat::FalloffFarMult, 1.f);
	const float HeadshotMult = GetStat(GBStat::HeadshotMult, 1.f);

	FCollisionQueryParams Params(SCENE_QUERY_STAT(GBGunTrace), false, Player);
	Params.AddIgnoredActor(this);

	for (int32 Index = 0; Index < Pellets; ++Index)
	{
		const FVector Direction = UGBCombatLibrary::ApplySpread(AimDirection, SpreadDegrees, Random);
		const FVector End = Origin + Direction * Range;

		FHitResult Hit;
		const bool bHit = World->LineTraceSingleByChannel(Hit, Origin, End, GBWeapon::AimTraceChannel, Params);
		AActor* Target = bHit ? Hit.GetActor() : nullptr;

		if (Target && UGBAttributeComponent::Find(Target))
		{
			FGBDamageInfo Info = BaseInfo;
			Info.HitLocation = Hit.ImpactPoint;
			Info.HitDirection = Direction;
			Info.Amount *= UGBCombatLibrary::DistanceFalloffMultiplier(Hit.Distance, FalloffStart, FalloffEnd, NearMult, FarMult);
			UGBCombatLibrary::ApplyWeakPointBonus(Target, Info, HeadshotMult);
			UGBCombatLibrary::DealDamage(Target, Info);
		}

#if !UE_BUILD_SHIPPING
		// Placeholder tracer.
		DrawDebugLine(World, Muzzle, bHit ? Hit.ImpactPoint : End, FColor(255, 220, 120), false, 0.05f, 0, 0.6f);
#endif
	}
}

void AGBGun::FireProjectile(const FGBDamageInfo& BaseInfo, float SpreadDegrees)
{
	AGBPlayerCharacter* Player = GetPlayerOwner();
	UWorld* World = GetWorld();

	const float Range = GetStat(GBStat::Range, 5000.f);
	const FVector Muzzle = Player->GetMuzzleLocation();
	FVector AimDirection = (FindAimPoint(Range) - Muzzle).GetSafeNormal();
	if (AimDirection.IsNearlyZero())
	{
		AimDirection = Player->GetAimDirection();
	}

	FGBProjectileConfig Config;
	Config.Damage = BaseInfo;
	Config.Speed = GetStat(GBStat::ProjectileSpeed, 3000.f);
	Config.Radius = GetStat(GBStat::ProjectileRadius, 5.f);
	Config.GravityScale = GetStat(GBStat::ProjectileGravity, 0.f);
	Config.MaxRange = Range;
	Config.bPixelArt = false; // art rule: gun shots are 3D
	Config.Color = GBPlaceholder::ElementColor(static_cast<uint8>(BaseInfo.Element));

	const int32 Pellets = FMath::Max(1, FMath::RoundToInt(GetStat(GBStat::Pellets, 1.f)));
	for (int32 Index = 0; Index < Pellets; ++Index)
	{
		const FVector Direction = UGBCombatLibrary::ApplySpread(AimDirection, SpreadDegrees, Random);
		AGBProjectile::SpawnProjectile(World, Config, Muzzle, Direction, Player);
	}
}

codex
I found a timing mismatch: Ignite has its own duration timer, but the interval-ticked elemental meter can remove it before its final damage tick. I also confirmed that the three `ElementalDefault.*DamageTaken` values in `Game.json` are never used by the damage calculation. I’m checking the remaining cleanup and source paths before finalizing the findings.

+	 * is decoupled from the build-up bonus (FGBDamageInfo::WeakPointBuildUpMult).
 	 */
 	static bool ApplyWeakPointBonus(const AActor* Target, FGBDamageInfo& Info, float WeakPointMult);
 
+	/** True when HitLocation is on Target's weak point (today: head of an AGBEnemyCharacter). The one place to add other weak points later. */
+	static bool IsWeakPointHit(const AActor* Target, const FVector& HitLocation);
+
+	/** Global counter for build-up events (one per shot, cast, blast or tick); never returns 0. */
+	static int32 NewBuildUpEventId();
+
+	/**
+	 * Sends Info.BuildUp to Target's elemental status (weapon weak point bonus included).
+	 * Returns the raw amount sent. Does nothing unless Info.BuildUp > 0, the element is
+	 * Fire / Lightning / Ice, CanDamage(Instigator, Target) and the target has a status component.
+	 */
+	static float ApplyBuildUp(AActor* Target, const FGBDamageInfo& Info);
+
 	/**
 	 * Aimed melee point (pure, tested): the point of the body segment BodyBottom..BodyTop (feet to top of head)
 	 * closest to the aim segment AimOrigin..AimOrigin + AimDirection * AimLength (FMath::SegmentDistToSegmentSafe).

 /**
  * How every tunable number is read in the game:
- *   value = Evaluate(Key, Base)  ->  (Base + sum of Add) * product of Multiply, or the last Override if any.
+ *   value = Evaluate(Key, Base)  ->  (Base + sum of Add) * product of Multiply (including the lowest MultiplyMin),
+ *   or the last Override if any.
  * - Actor stats (MoveSpeed, MaxHealth...): base values are stored here with SetBaseStats and read with GetStat.
  * - Weapon and spell numbers: the base comes from the weapon / spell definition and the caller passes the
  *   modifier keys ("Weapon.<Id>.<Stat>", "Weapon.All.<Stat>", "Spell.<Id>.<Param>", "Spell.All.<Param>").
  * Modifiers are added in groups identified by a Source name ("Skill.FireballDamage", "Armor", "Effect.Slow#3",
  * "Passive.DoubleJump"), and removed by that same name. Skill trees, armor, status effects, air passives and
- * the weapon enchant are all just sources of modifiers.
+ * the weapon enchant are all just sources of modifiers. MultiplyMin is like Multiply, but only the lowest
+ * one on a stat counts (the strongest slow wins); it multiplies with the plain Multiply ones.

 UCLASS(ClassGroup = (Glyphbound), meta = (BlueprintSpawnableComponent))
 class GLYPHBOUND_API UGBStatsComponent : public UActorComponent
// Pure elemental build-up logic (GDD 6): one bar per element, no UObjects, unit-tested.
#pragma once

#include "CoreMinimal.h"
#include "GBBuildUpMeter.generated.h"

UENUM(BlueprintType)
enum class EGBBuildUpPhase : uint8
{
	Building,
	Active,
	Immune
};

/** Bit mask returned by FGBBuildUpMeter::Advance. */
enum class EGBBuildUpEvent : uint8
{
	ValueChanged = 1 << 0,
	StatusEnded = 1 << 1,
	ImmunityEnded = 1 << 2
};

USTRUCT(BlueprintType)
struct GLYPHBOUND_API FGBBuildUpConfig
{
	GENERATED_BODY()

	/** <= 0: this bar never fills (element disabled for this enemy). */
	UPROPERTY() float Threshold = 0.f;
	/** Multiplier on incoming build-up (resistance < 1, vulnerability > 1, 0 = immune). */
	UPROPERTY() float BuildUpTaken = 1.f;
	/** Seconds without build-up before the bar drains. */
	UPROPERTY() float DecayDelay = 0.f;
	/** Points per second. */
	UPROPERTY() float DecayRate = 0.f;
	UPROPERTY() float StatusDuration = 0.f;
	UPROPERTY() float ImmunityDuration = 0.f;
};

USTRUCT(BlueprintType)
struct GLYPHBOUND_API FGBBuildUpMeter
{
	GENERATED_BODY()

	UPROPERTY() float Value = 0.f;
	UPROPERTY() EGBBuildUpPhase Phase = EGBBuildUpPhase::Building;
	UPROPERTY() float PhaseTimeRemaining = 0.f;
	UPROPERTY() float TimeSinceBuildUp = 0.f;

	/**
	 * Adds Amount * BuildUpTaken while Building. Returns true when this add filled the bar:
	 * Value goes to 0 and the phase to Active for StatusDuration. Ignored (false) when not Building,
	 * Amount <= 0, BuildUpTaken <= 0 or Threshold <= 0. Resets the decay delay.
	 * Full means Value >= Threshold - 1e-4 (float tolerance, so exactly the threshold counts).
	 */
	bool Add(float Amount, const FGBBuildUpConfig& Config);

	/** Advances time; returns a bit mask of EGBBuildUpEvent (ValueChanged, StatusEnded, ImmunityEnded). */
	uint8 Advance(float DeltaTime, const FGBBuildUpConfig& Config);

	/** Building with an empty bar: nothing to tick. */
	bool IsIdle() const;
};

/**
 * Remembers the last 8 build-up events of one target, so one event (a shotgun shot) counts once:
 * the biggest amount of that event wins.
 */
struct GLYPHBOUND_API FGBBuildUpEventLog
{

	/**
	 * Returns how much of Amount to really add: all of it for a new id (or id 0);
	 * for a known id only the part above what that id already added (0 if smaller).
	 */
	float Accept(int32 EventId, float Amount);

private:
	struct FEntry
	{
		int32 Id = 0;
		float Amount = 0.f;
	};
	FEntry Entries[8] = {};
	int32 NextSlot = 0;
};

bool AGBSpellActor::IsHostileTarget(const AActor* Actor) const
{
	if (!Actor || Actor == this || Actor == GetCaster())
	{
		return false;
	}
	if (!UGBAttributeComponent::Find(Actor) || !UGBCombatLibrary::IsAlive(Actor))
	{
		return false;
	}
	return UGBCombatLibrary::AreHostile(GetCaster(), Actor);
}

void AGBSpellActor::QueryHostiles(const FVector& Center, const FQuat& Rotation, const FCollisionShape& Shape, TArray<AActor*>& OutActors) const
{
	OutActors.Reset();
	UWorld* World = GetWorld();
	if (!World)
	{
		return;
	}
	FCollisionObjectQueryParams ObjectParams;
	ObjectParams.AddObjectTypesToQuery(ECC_Pawn);
	ObjectParams.AddObjectTypesToQuery(ECC_WorldDynamic);
	FCollisionQueryParams Params(SCENE_QUERY_STAT(GBSpellArea), false, this);

	TArray<FOverlapResult> Overlaps;
	World->OverlapMultiByObjectType(Overlaps, Center, Rotation, ObjectParams, Shape, Params);
	for (const FOverlapResult& Overlap : Overlaps)
	{
		AActor* Actor = Overlap.GetActor();
		if (IsHostileTarget(Actor))
		{
			OutActors.AddUnique(Actor);
		}
	}
}

void AGBSpellActor::GatherHostilesInSphere(const FVector& Center, float Radius, TArray<AActor*>& OutActors) const
{
	OutActors.Reset();
	if (Radius <= 0.f)
	{
		return;
	}
	QueryHostiles(Center, FQuat::Identity, FCollisionShape::MakeSphere(Radius), OutActors);
}

void AGBSpellActor::GatherHostilesOnDisc(const FVector& GroundCenter, float Radius, float Height, TArray<AActor*>& OutActors) const
{
	OutActors.Reset();
	if (Radius <= 0.f || Height <= 0.f)
	{
		return;
	}
	const FVector BoxCenter = GroundCenter + FVector(0.f, 0.f, Height * 0.5f);
	QueryHostiles(BoxCenter, FQuat::Identity, FCollisionShape::MakeBox(FVector(Radius, Radius, Height * 0.5f)), OutActors);
	// The box is only the broad phase: keep actors whose collision touches the disc horizontally.
	OutActors.RemoveAll([&GroundCenter, Radius](const AActor* Actor)
	{
		return FVector::Dist2D(Actor->GetActorLocation(), GroundCenter) > Radius + Actor->GetSimpleCollisionRadius();
	});
}

void AGBSpellActor::GatherHostilesInBox(const FVector& Center, const FQuat& Rotation, const FVector& Extent, TArray<AActor*>& OutActors) const
{
	OutActors.Reset();
	if (Extent.GetMin() <= 0.f)
	{
		return;
	}
	QueryHostiles(Center, Rotation, FCollisionShape::MakeBox(Extent), OutActors);
}

		{
			NextWaveTimer -= DeltaSeconds;
			if (NextWaveTimer <= 0.f)
			{
				NextWaveTimer = -1.f;
				SpawnWave(CurrentWave + 1);
			}
			break;
		}

		// Enemies removed without dying (fell out of the world, destroyed by a script) do not block the wave.
		AliveEnemies.RemoveAll([](const TObjectPtr<AGBEnemyCharacter>& Enemy) { return !IsValid(Enemy) || Enemy->IsDead(); });
		if (AliveEnemies.Num() == 0)

			HandleWaveDefeated();
		}
		break;
	}

	case EGBEncounterState::Cleared:
	default:
		SetActorTickEnabled(false);
		break;
	}
}

void AGBEncounter::Clear()
{
	if (State == EGBEncounterState::Cleared)
	{
		return;
	}

	State = EGBEncounterState::Cleared;
	NextWaveTimer = -1.f;

	const FGBEncounterDefinition* Def = FindDefinition();
	const int32 Points = Def ? FMath::Max(0, Def->SkillPoints) : 0;
	const TArray<FGBDropDefinition> Rewards = Def ? Def->Rewards : TArray<FGBDropDefinition>();

	UGBProgressSubsystem* Progress = UGBProgressSubsystem::Get(this);
	if (Progress)
	{
		Progress->SetFlag(GetClearedFlag(), true);
	}

	AGBPlayerCharacter* Player = AGBPlayerCharacter::Get(this);
	if (Player && Points > 0)
	{
		if (UGBSkillTreeComponent* SkillTree = Player->GetSkillTree())
		{
			SkillTree->AddPoints(Points);
		}
	}

	if (Rewards.Num() > 0)
	{
		AGBPickup::SpawnDrops(GetWorld(), Rewards, GetActorLocation());
	}

	if (AGBHUD* HUD = AGBHUD::Get(this))
	{
		const FString Text = Points > 0
			? FString::Printf(TEXT("Encounter cleared: +%d skill point%s"), Points, Points == 1 ? TEXT("") : TEXT("s"))
			: FString(TEXT("Encounter cleared"));
		HUD->ShowMessage(Text);
	}

	if (Progress && Player && !Player->IsDead())
	{
		Progress->SaveCheckpoint(Player);
	}

	OnCleared.Broadcast(this);
	SetActorTickEnabled(false);
}

void AGBEncounter::ResetEncounter()
{
	if (State == EGBEncounterState::Cleared)
	{
		return;
	}

	for (const TObjectPtr<AGBEnemyCharacter>& Enemy : AliveEnemies)
	{
		if (IsValid(Enemy))
		{
			Enemy->OnDied.RemoveAll(this);
			Enemy->Destroy();
		}
	}
	AliveEnemies.Reset();

	State = EGBEncounterState::Waiting;
	CurrentWave = -1;
	NextWaveTimer = -1.f;
	SetActorTickEnabled(true);
}
			}
			if (!Dummy)
			{
				const FVector At = Player->GetActorLocation() + Player->GetAimDirection() * 500.f;
				Dummy = AGBEnemyCharacter::SpawnEnemy(World, FName(TEXT("TrainingDummy")), At, FRotator::ZeroRotator, EGBEraPresence::Both);
			}
			if (!Dummy || Dummy->IsDead())
			{
				UE_LOG(LogGB, Error, TEXT("SMOKE elemental: no living TrainingDummy"));
				return;
			}
			UGBElementalStatusComponent* Elemental = Dummy->GetElementalStatus();
			if (!Elemental)
			{
				UE_LOG(LogGB, Error, TEXT("SMOKE elemental: dummy has no status component"));
				return;
			}
			// Dedupe: two adds of 20 with the SAME event id leave 20 (times BuildUpTaken, 1 by default).
			const int32 DedupeId = UGBCombatLibrary::NewBuildUpEventId();
			FGBDamageInfo First;
			First.Element = EGBElement::Fire;
			First.Instigator = Player;
			First.BuildUp = 20.f;
			First.BuildUpEventId = DedupeId;
			UGBCombatLibrary::ApplyBuildUp(Dummy, First);
			UGBCombatLibrary::ApplyBuildUp(Dummy, First);
			const float AfterDedupe = Elemental->GetBuildUp(EGBElement::Fire);
			const bool bDedupeOk = FMath::IsNearlyEqual(AfterDedupe, 20.f, 1.e-2f);
			UE_LOG(LogGB, Display, TEXT("SMOKE elemental dedupe ok=%d"), bDedupeOk ? 1 : 0);
			if (!bDedupeOk)
			{
				UE_LOG(LogGB, Error, TEXT("SMOKE elemental dedupe FAILED: fire bar is %.2f, expected 20"), AfterDedupe);
			}
			// Clear the fire bar before the per-element trigger checks (fresh decay via ClearAll).
			Elemental->ClearAll();

			const EGBElement Elems[3] = { EGBElement::Fire, EGBElement::Lightning, EGBElement::Ice };
			const TCHAR* Names[3] = { TEXT("Fire"), TEXT("Lightning"), TEXT("Ice") };
			for (int32 i = 0; i < 3; ++i)
			{
				Elemental->ClearAll();
				bool bTriggered = false;
				int32 Adds = 0;
				for (int32 n = 0; n < 10 && !bTriggered; ++n)
				{
					FGBDamageInfo Info;
					Info.Element = Elems[i];
					Info.Instigator = Player;
					Info.BuildUp = 30.f;
					Info.BuildUpEventId = UGBCombatLibrary::NewBuildUpEventId();
					UGBCombatLibrary::ApplyBuildUp(Dummy, Info);
					++Adds;
					bTriggered = Elemental->IsStatusActive(Elems[i]);
				}
				UE_LOG(LogGB, Display, TEXT("SMOKE elemental %s triggered=%d adds=%d"), Names[i], bTriggered ? 1 : 0, Adds);
				if (!bTriggered)
				{
					UE_LOG(LogGB, Error, TEXT("SMOKE elemental %s FAILED to trigger"), Names[i]);
					continue;
				}
				if (Elems[i] == EGBElement::Fire)
				{
					UGBStatusEffectComponent* Effects = Dummy->GetStatusEffects();
					const bool bIgnite = Effects && Effects->HasEffect(FName(TEXT("Ignite")));
					UE_LOG(LogGB, Display, TEXT("SMOKE elemental Fire ignite=%d"), bIgnite ? 1 : 0);
					if (!bIgnite)
					{
						UE_LOG(LogGB, Error, TEXT("SMOKE elemental Fire: Ignite effect missing"));
					}
				}
				FGBDamageInfo Extra;
				Extra.Element = Elems[i];
				Extra.Instigator = Player;
				Extra.BuildUp = 30.f;
				Extra.BuildUpEventId = UGBCombatLibrary::NewBuildUpEventId();
				const float Before = Elemental->GetBuildUp(Elems[i]);
				UGBCombatLibrary::ApplyBuildUp(Dummy, Extra);
				const float After = Elemental->GetBuildUp(Elems[i]);
				const bool bBlocked = Elemental->IsStatusActive(Elems[i]) && FMath::IsNearlyEqual(Before, After, 1.e-3f);
				UE_LOG(LogGB, Display, TEXT("SMOKE elemental %s blockedWhileActive=%d"), Names[i], bBlocked ? 1 : 0);
				if (!bBlocked)
				{
					UE_LOG(LogGB, Error, TEXT("SMOKE elemental %s: add during status was not refused"), Names[i]);
				}
			}
			Elemental->ClearAll();
		});
		Add(50.5f + S, TEXT("quit"), [](UWorld*, AGBPlayerCharacter*)
		{
			UE_LOG(LogGB, Display, TEXT("SMOKE DONE"));
			FPlatformMisc::RequestExit(false);
		});
	}

IMPLEMENT_SIMPLE_AUTOMATION_TEST(FGBElementalMeterDecayTest, "Glyphbound.Elemental.MeterDecay",
	EAutomationTestFlags::EditorContext | EAutomationTestFlags::EngineFilter)
bool FGBElementalMeterDecayTest::RunTest(const FString& Parameters)
{
	FGBBuildUpConfig Config;
	Config.Threshold = 1000.f;
	Config.BuildUpTaken = 1.f;
	Config.DecayDelay = 2.f;
	Config.DecayRate = 20.f;
	Config.StatusDuration = 1.f;

	FGBBuildUpMeter Meter;
	Meter.Add(100.f, Config);
	Meter.Advance(1.f, Config);
	TestNearlyEqual(TEXT("Nothing drains during the delay"), Meter.Value, 100.f, 1.e-3f);
	Meter.Advance(1.5f, Config);
	// TimeSince is 2.5, 0.5 s past the delay: 100 - 20*0.5 = 90.
	TestNearlyEqual(TEXT("Drains at DecayRate after the delay"), Meter.Value, 90.f, 1.e-3f);

	// One Advance of 1.0 s equals ten steps of 0.1 s, crossing the delay boundary.
	FGBBuildUpMeter One;
	FGBBuildUpMeter Ten;
	One.Add(100.f, Config);
	Ten.Add(100.f, Config);
	One.Advance(1.5f, Config);
	Ten.Advance(1.5f, Config);
	One.Advance(1.f, Config);
	for (int32 i = 0; i < 10; ++i)
	{
		Ten.Advance(0.1f, Config);
	}
	TestNearlyEqual(TEXT("1.0 s equals ten 0.1 s steps across the delay"), One.Value, Ten.Value, 1.e-3f);

	// Fully after the delay.
	FGBBuildUpMeter One2;
	FGBBuildUpMeter Ten2;
	One2.Add(100.f, Config);
	Ten2.Add(100.f, Config);
	One2.Advance(3.f, Config);
	Ten2.Advance(3.f, Config);
	One2.Advance(1.f, Config);
	for (int32 i = 0; i < 10; ++i)
	{
		Ten2.Advance(0.1f, Config);
	}
	TestNearlyEqual(TEXT("1.0 s equals ten 0.1 s steps after the delay"), One2.Value, Ten2.Value, 1.e-3f);

	// Never below 0.
	FGBBuildUpMeter Floor;
	Floor.Add(10.f, Config);
	Floor.Advance(100.f, Config);
	TestNearlyEqual(TEXT("Never below 0"), Floor.Value, 0.f, 1.e-3f);

	// An add resets the delay.
	FGBBuildUpMeter Reset;
	Reset.Add(100.f, Config);
	Reset.Advance(1.9f, Config);
	Reset.Add(10.f, Config);
	Reset.Advance(1.f, Config);
	// 1.0 s after the second add is still inside the 2 s delay: 110, no drain.
	TestNearlyEqual(TEXT("An add resets the delay"), Reset.Value, 110.f, 1.e-3f);
	return true;
}


codex
1. **[Combat/GBElementalStatusComponent.cpp:431](C:/GameJam/Glyphbound/Source/Glyphbound/Combat/GBElementalStatusComponent.cpp:431), also lines 203–207 and 315 — Ignite can lose its final damage tick.** When another bar already keeps the component ticking, a status started between ticks is advanced by the entire next interval, including time before it started. Its early expiry calls `RemoveEffect("Ignite")`, potentially delivering seven ticks (42 damage) instead of eight (48), before modifiers. **Smallest fix:** advance meters to the hit’s timestamp before changing them, and let the status-effect component finish Ignite’s final tick before removing it.

2. **[Stats/GBAttributeComponent.cpp:96–99](C:/GameJam/Glyphbound/Source/Glyphbound/Stats/GBAttributeComponent.cpp:96) — Elemental damage defaults are ignored.** The calculation falls back directly to `1.f`; it never reads `Game.json`’s `ElementalDefault.FireDamageTaken`, `LightningDamageTaken`, or `IceDamageTaken`. Changing those advertised tuning values—including in the packaged game—has no effect on enemies without explicit overrides. **Smallest fix:** use the corresponding game value as the fallback passed to `GetStat`, retaining the enemy override and modifiers.

3. **[Combat/GBBuildUpMeter.cpp:62–65](C:/GameJam/Glyphbound/Source/Glyphbound/Combat/GBBuildUpMeter.cpp:62) — A large time step can leave an already-expired immunity active.** Overshoot carries into `PhaseTimeRemaining`, but the function leaves the phase `Immune` even when that remainder is negative; only another `Advance` clears it. After a hitch, or with short configured durations, hits are therefore rejected for an extra component tick, and one large step differs from equivalent smaller steps. **Smallest fix:** immediately transition to `Building` and emit `ImmunityEnded` when the carried immunity remainder is already exhausted.

I would fix Ignite timing first, the ignored damage defaults second, and immunity overshoot third. Static inspection found shotgun deduplication, piercing and blast delivery, nested Shock death handling and encounter notification, windup cancellation, era pause/resume, strongest-slow selection, Deep Freeze/QTE scaling and its existing cap solid. No files were modified, and nothing was built or run.

2. **[Stats/GBAttributeComponent.cpp:96–99](C:/GameJam/Glyphbound/Source/Glyphbound/Stats/GBAttributeComponent.cpp:96) — Elemental damage defaults are ignored.** The calculation falls back directly to `1.f`; it never reads `Game.json`’s `ElementalDefault.FireDamageTaken`, `LightningDamageTaken`, or `IceDamageTaken`. Changing those advertised tuning values—including in the packaged game—has no effect on enemies without explicit overrides. **Smallest fix:** use the corresponding game value as the fallback passed to `GetStat`, retaining the enemy override and modifiers.

3. **[Combat/GBBuildUpMeter.cpp:62–65](C:/GameJam/Glyphbound/Source/Glyphbound/Combat/GBBuildUpMeter.cpp:62) — A large time step can leave an already-expired immunity active.** Overshoot carries into `PhaseTimeRemaining`, but the function leaves the phase `Immune` even when that remainder is negative; only another `Advance` clears it. After a hitch, or with short configured durations, hits are therefore rejected for an extra component tick, and one large step differs from equivalent smaller steps. **Smallest fix:** immediately transition to `Building` and emit `ImmunityEnded` when the carried immunity remainder is already exhausted.

I would fix Ignite timing first, the ignored damage defaults second, and immunity overshoot third. Static inspection found shotgun deduplication, piercing and blast delivery, nested Shock death handling and encounter notification, windup cancellation, era pause/resume, strongest-slow selection, Deep Freeze/QTE scaling and its existing cap solid. No files were modified, and nothing was built or run.
